## Approved Plan:
# Content Expansion Plan — Cow/Pig, Coal, Creeper/TNT, Villagers

## Context

The user asked whether the game needs more mobs/items/blocks/tools/weapons (Creepers, Villagers, Coal, Iron Ore, Crafting Tables) to be more fun. Findings from exploring the codebase:

- The game is **already content-rich**: 48 blocks, 62 items, 48 recipes, 7 pickaxe tiers, 8 sword tiers, 7 spear tiers, a bow, 6 armor pieces, 5 ore tiers, 7 mobs incl. a summonable boss, farming, breeding, dungeons, per-voxel lighting.
- It is **not short on gear or ore tiers** — adding more swords/ores has diminishing returns. The fun gaps are **new mechanics and creature variety**.
- Mapping the user's list: **Iron Ore already exists as Sliver Ore** (tier-2, stone-pickaxe-mineable) — don't duplicate it. **Crafting Tables don't fit** — crafting is intentionally always-available via the recipe book; a table gate is a *restriction*, not fun. **Coal, Creepers, Villagers are real, worthwhile gaps.**

The user chose to plan **all four** of: Cow+Pig, Coal & fuel economy, Creeper+TNT, Villagers+trading. This plan phases them by fun-per-effort and dependency. **Zero-binary-assets principle holds throughout** — every texture/sprite/sound is generated procedurally.

**Key architectural realization:** the explosion system (Phase 3) is *not* a new subsystem — it's a batched loop over the existing `blockChanges.set()` chokepoint (which auto-relights each edit) + the existing `applyDamage`/`removeMobAt` deps, finished with one `worldMeshDirty = true`. Destroying 40 blocks costs 40 cheap localized relights + exactly ONE remesh — the same remesh a single mined block already pays.

**Exhaustive `Record<MobKind>` tables** (TypeScript strict forces an entry per new mob — a useful checklist): `MOB_TEMPLATES` (`mobs.ts`), `MOB_DROPS` (`mobLoot.ts`), `CALL_INTERVALS` (`mobAmbience.ts`), `MOB_AMBIENT_SOUNDS` + `MOB_ATTACK_SOUNDS` (`soundParams.ts`), and the `MobKind` union (`types.ts`).

---

## Phase 1 — Cow + Pig (quick win, pure template-follow)

**Goal:** two passive mobs cloning the sheep/chicken pattern; new meat foods; leather generalized off horse.

**Changes:**
- `lib/game/types.ts` — add `"cow" | "pig"` to `MobKind`.
- `lib/game/mobs.ts` — two `MOB_TEMPLATES` entries (cow: white/brown, `hp:10, speed:0.9, attackDamage:0`; pig: pink, `hp:8, speed:1.0`). No new model code — `createMobModel` is fully parameterized by the 7-tuple `modelArgs`.
- `lib/game/mobLoot.ts` — `cow: [leather 1-2, raw_beef 1]`, `pig: [raw_porkchop 1]`.
- `lib/game/items.ts` — new foods `raw_beef`(7? use hunger 3), `cooked_beef`(8), `raw_porkchop`(3), `cooked_porkchop`(8). `leather` already exists.
- `lib/game/recipes.ts` — `cook_beef`, `cook_porkchop` (furnace, follow `cook_chicken`). **If Phase 2 ships first, author these with coal fuel directly; else use `planks` per current convention and retrofit in Phase 2.**
- `interact.ts` `FEED_ITEMS` — `cow: "wheat"`, `pig: "seeds"` (carrots don't exist).
- `spawnDirector.ts` `spawnInitialMobs` — add cow×4, pig×4 to the passive ring.
- Fill the exhaustive `Record<MobKind>` sound/ambience tables (procedural moo/oink zzfx tuples).

**Save/worldgen:** none. Mobs + breeding are transient; new items persist by string id. No `WORLDGEN_VERSION` bump, schema stays v5.

**Tests/docs:** extend `mobLoot.test.ts`, `breeding.test.ts`, recipe test for the cook recipes. Docs: `reference.md`, `manual.md` (`adding-content.md` is the template, no change needed).

**Decision (default chosen):** rely on **breeding** for repopulation rather than adding a passive spawn director — matches current sheep/chicken/horse behavior (initial-spawn + breed only).

---

## Phase 2 — Coal & fuel economy

**Goal:** a real early-game resource and a reason to mine shallow; deepen the thin furnace economy.

**Approach decision — Option (b), lightweight (recommended).** The furnace has **zero block-entity state** — `interactFurnace` just sets `state.craftingStation = "furnace"` and smelting recipes are ordinary `RECIPES` with `station:"furnace"` consuming a plank "fuel" ingredient instantly. A real fuel-slot/burn-timer (Option a) would require a furnace block-entity, a smelting tick system, **new serialized save state**, and a redesigned furnace panel — multi-day for a fuel meter. Option (b) reuses the existing recipe machinery and ships in hours.

**Changes:**
- `lib/world/blocks.ts` — new `CoalOre` block (dark speckled atlas tile in `atlas.ts`); add to `BLOCK_COLORS`, `HELD_BLOCK_COLORS`, `BREAK_HARDNESS` (~6), `BLOCK_TO_SLOT` (→ `"coal"`).
- `lib/game/engine/systems/mining.ts` `canMineBlock` — add `if (block === BlockId.CoalOre) return toolTier >= 1;` (wood-pickaxe gate; everything not explicitly listed is currently hand-mineable).
- `lib/game/items.ts` — new items `coal`, `charcoal` (interchangeable fuel materials).
- `lib/world/generation.ts` `GEN.oreConfigs` — add CoalOre **first in the array** (most common), shallow, high attempts (~160k). Re-derive exact params against the determinism test; keep it below the surface band so surface probes stay green.
- `lib/game/recipes.ts` — `charcoal` (smelt 1 wood → 1 charcoal, furnace); retarget cook recipes from `planks` to coal/charcoal (add `cook_*_coal` + `cook_*_charcoal` variants since `Recipe.cost` has no OR); change `torch` recipe `1 wood → 4 torch` to `1 coal + 1 wood → 4 torch`.

**Save/worldgen:** **new ore in `generateWorld` ⇒ determinism re-baseline + bump `WORLDGEN_VERSION` 7 → 8** (`config.ts`). Affected worlds reboot from seed; schema stays v5 (coal/charcoal additive, CoalOre persists via block-diff). Re-roll `generation.test.ts` block-hash + meshing snapshot.

**Tests/docs:** update pinned digests; probe CoalOre is shallow + more common than sliver; `canMineBlock` tier test; recipe tests. Docs: `save-format.md` (WORLDGEN_VERSION-8 bullet, mirror the lava entry), `tuning.md` (ore params + torch change), `reference.md`, `manual.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

**Decision (default chosen):** **torch now requires coal** (Minecraft-style early-game tension) — flag as a balance change; revert to wood-only if the user dislikes the gate.

---

## Phase 3 — Creeper + TNT (the novel explosion system)

**Goal:** the biggest "Minecraft feel" gain — a new threat *and* a demolition tool off one reusable explosion primitive.

### 3a. Explosion primitive — new `lib/game/engine/systems/explosion.ts`

`explode(state, cx, cy, cz, { power }, deps)` where `deps` is the existing `mobTickDeps` shape (`applyDamage`, `removeMobAt`, `surfaceYAt`, `rng`, `emit`):
1. **Destroy blocks:** iterate the sphere `dist <= power`; introduce `blastResistance(block)` (`Partial<Record<BlockId,number>>`, default low; Bedrock/Spawner/Lava = Infinity; Stone/Cobble/Brick high-but-finite); destroy when `power*(1 - dist/power) > blastResistance`. Each destroyed cell → `state.blockChanges.set(x,y,z, Air)` (auto-relights + records delta). **Set `worldMeshDirty = true` exactly once after the loop**, never inside it. Mirror `mining.ts` door-pair removal for door cells.
2. **Player damage:** falloff `damage = round(maxDamage * (1 - dist/(power*2)))` via `deps.applyDamage` (armor-aware); knockback the player velocity away from center (mirror `mobAI.ts` knockback).
3. **Mob damage:** loop `state.mobs`, falloff damage + knockback; collect dead indices, call `deps.removeMobAt` **after** the loop in descending index order (existing pattern). Chaining is natural and fun.
4. **Emit** a new `{ type:"explosion", x, y, z, power }` `GameEvent` → renderer smoke/fire `emitBurst` scaled by power + synthesized `EXPLOSION_SOUND`.

It touches nothing new — reuses `blockChanges`, `applyDamage`/`removeMobAt`, one `worldMeshDirty`.

### 3b. Creeper mob
- `types.ts` `MobKind += "creeper"`; `MobState` += transient `fuseTimer` (free — never serialized); init in `pushMob`.
- `mobs.ts` — green, `hostile`, `hp:10, speed:1.0, detectRange:11, attackDamage:0`; optional template fields `explodes:true, fuseSeconds:1.5, fuseRange:2.5, explosionPower:3`.
- `mobAI.ts` — reuse existing hostile chase; when within `fuseRange` start fuse (emit hiss via `mobAttacked`); tick down; on `<=0` call `explode(...)` and mark creeper dead (dies in its blast, drops gunpowder via `removeMobAt`). Killing mid-fuse needs no special code (hp→0 path already removes + drops). Add `explode` to `MobTickDeps`, wire in `GameEngine.mobTickDeps`. Include creeper in the daylight-burn clause + night spawn lists.
- `mobLoot.ts` — `creeper: [gunpowder 1-2]`. Fill exhaustive sound/ambience tables (hiss as attack sound).

### 3c. TNT + gunpowder
- `items.ts` — `gunpowder` (material), `tnt` (block item). `blocks.ts` — `Tnt` block (red/white striped tile, low hardness, normal placeable/minable until ignited).
- `recipes.ts` — `tnt`: `4 gunpowder + 1 sand → 1 tnt`.
- **Ignition:** in `interact.ts` `tryUseHeldItem`, if held item is `"torch"` and aimed block is `Tnt`, prime it (zero new item — avoids inventing flint&steel).
- **Priming (recommended v1):** session-only `primedTnt: Map<voxelIndex, fuseTimer>` on `GameState` (transient like containers); new `tickPrimedTnt` system ticks down, on expiry sets the block to Air + calls `explode` at block center. **Chaining:** the destruction loop special-cases `Tnt` — instead of Air, prime it with a short random fuse.
- Wire `tickPrimedTnt` into `GameEngine.step` after `tickMobs`.

### 3d. Presentation (zero-asset)
- `mobVisuals.ts` — creeper swells + flashes white while `fuseTimer > 0`.
- `GameRenderer.ts` event switch — `explosion` → big smoke/fire burst.
- `audioDirector.ts` / `soundParams.ts` — `EXPLOSION_SOUND` boom, creeper hiss + ambient zzfx tuples.

**Save/worldgen:** **none.** Creeper spawned, TNT crafted (boss precedent). Primed-TNT + creeper fuse are transient. Schema v5; document in `save-format.md` that a save mid-fuse loads with TNT un-primed / no creeper.

**Tests/docs:** new `explosion.test.ts` (headless: clears expected sphere, Bedrock/Spawner intact, falloff damage+knockback, one event, `worldMeshDirty` set once); creeper AI in `mobAI.test.ts`; TNT ignite/detonate/chain; loot+recipe tests. Docs: `adding-content.md` (document the explosion primitive as a reusable system), `reference.md`, `manual.md`, `tuning.md` (power/fuse/blast-resistance constants in `config.ts`), `save-format.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

**Decisions (defaults chosen):** no loot from blasted blocks; creeper fuse **aborts** if player flees; **torch** ignition; TNT **chains**; primed-TNT as block-map (not entity) for v1.

---

## Phase 4 — Villagers + trading (the big arc; sub-phase 4a→4b→4c)

**Goal:** give exploration a social/economy purpose. Ships last (biggest, and its worldgen bump is cleanest applied once).

### 4a. Village structures
- Reuse `placeHouse`/`placeStructures` (`generation.ts`), **not** dungeons — houses already build above-ground on validated flat ground. Add `placeVillages(world, rand)` placing N houses in a ring around a center; call after `placeStructures`, before `placeDungeons`.
- **Determinism:** villagers must spawn at village sites on load *without reading blocks*. Copy the **dungeon precedent** — add `collectVillageSites(world, worldType)` replaying placement math against a seed-only PRNG + seed-pure `terrainTopY` (mirror `collectDungeonSites`), store anchors in a session-only set on `GameState`.
- **Worldgen change ⇒ re-baseline + `WORLDGEN_VERSION` bump** (8 → 9). **This is the first surface-altering re-baseline** — update the "surface untouched" structural probes, not just the hashes. Schema stays v5.

### 4b. Villager mob
- `types.ts` `MobKind += "villager"`; `mobs.ts` — passive but **does NOT flee** (add `wandersNeutral` flag; `mobAI.ts` skips the flee branch for it — gate strictly so sheep/cow/pig flee unchanged). `hp:20, speed:0.6, attackDamage:0`.
- `mobLoot.ts` — `villager: []`. Spawn at village anchors on load via new `spawnVillageMobs` (constructor, like `spawnInitialMobs`), capped per village. Fill exhaustive sound tables (mumble).

### 4c. Trading economy + UI
- **Currency = `emerald` as a trade-only item** (recommended) — avoids a *second* worldgen bump that an emerald ore would force. New `emerald` material in `items.ts`.
- New `lib/game/trades.ts` — `TRADES: TradeOffer[]` (`{id, give[], get, maxUses}`); buy offers (emerald → goods) + sell offers (materials → emerald). The trade analog of `RECIPES`.
- **UI:** extend the `craftingStation` union to `"furnace" | "villager"` so the existing recipe-book panel machinery is reused. Right-click a villager (via `findAimedMobIndex` in `GameEngine.dispatch`) sets `tradingVillagerId` + opens inventory in trade mode. New `TradePanel` section modeled on the recipe-book block (reuse `ItemIcon`, `createSlot`, `canCraft`-style disabled state). New `{type:"trade", offerId}` command + a `dispatch` case mirroring `craft` (deduct gives, add get, decrement uses).
- **Restock:** session-only `tradeUses` on the villager `MobState`, refilled on a timer — transient/seeded like dungeon loot (reload = fresh stock), so **no save-format change**.

**Save/worldgen:** 4a bumps `WORLDGEN_VERSION` (surface baseline changes); 4b/4c transient (villagers respawn from seed anchors; emerald/items persist by string id; trade state session-only). Schema stays v5.

**Tests/docs:** `generation.test.ts` (new digests + "villages near expected centers" probe + `collectVillageSites` determinism test); `trades.test.ts`; villager-no-flee AI test; `TradePanel` component test. Docs: `adding-content.md` (village seed-anchor pattern + trade system), `reference.md`, `manual.md`, `tuning.md`, `save-format.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`.

**Decisions (defaults chosen):** emerald = **trade-item** not ore; trade-use state **transient** (no schema bump — persisting it would be a v6 change); villagers **respawn from seed anchors** (no persistence).

---

## Cross-cutting summary

| Phase | New MobKind | New blocks | Worldgen change | WORLDGEN_VERSION | Save schema |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 Cow/Pig | cow, pig | — | no | no bump | v5 |
| 2 Coal | — | CoalOre | **yes (new ore)** | **bump 7→8** | v5 |
| 3 Creeper/TNT | creeper | Tnt | no | no bump | v5 |
| 4 Villagers | villager | — (emerald=item) | **yes (villages, surface)** | **bump 8→9** | v5 |

**Sequencing:** 1 → 2 → 3 → 4. Phase 1 before 2 (Phase 2 retargets Phase 1's cook recipes to coal). Phase 4 last (4a before 4b before 4c).

**Git workflow (one branch, one PR — user's choice):** a single `feat/content-expansion` branch off `main` holding the whole arc, opened as one PR against `hutusi/main`. Commit in **focused slices — one Conventional-Commit per phase** (e.g. `feat(mobs): add cow and pig`, `feat(world): add coal ore and fuel economy`, `feat(combat): add creeper and TNT explosions`, `feat(world): add villages and trading`), each with a body explaining the why, and `bun run lint` green at every commit so the branch stays bisectable. If incremental review is later preferred, the per-phase commits split cleanly.

**Worldgen versioning — collapse to ONE bump:** Phase 2 (coal) and Phase 4a (villages) both alter worldgen determinism. Shipping them on one branch means bumping `WORLDGEN_VERSION` **once** to the final value (7 → 8), so affected worlds reset a single time rather than twice. Re-baseline `generation.test.ts` once, after both phases' worldgen code is in.

## Verification (per phase, before each PR)

- Gate (CI `verify`): `bun run lint`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun run format:check`, `bun test`, `bun run build` all green. `bun run test:e2e` for Phase 3 (renderer/explosion particles) and Phase 4 (trade UI).
- The exhaustive `Record<MobKind>` compile errors are the per-mob checklist — `bun run typecheck` won't pass until every table has the new entry.
- Phases 2 & 4a change worldgen determinism hashes — re-baseline `generation.test.ts` deliberately (confirm the diff is only the intended new ore/structures), and verify surface structural probes (untouched for coal, updated for villages).
- Manual gameplay pass (Phase 3 especially): craft TNT, ignite with a torch, confirm crater + chained detonation + damage falloff; let a creeper approach, confirm fuse hiss/swell, detonation, and that killing it mid-fuse drops gunpowder and cancels the blast. Phase 4: find a village, right-click a villager, complete a buy and a sell trade, confirm restock.
- Confirm docs + `CHANGELOG.md` updated for every touched tunable/system/mechanic/save-impact (part of the gate, not an afterthought).

77	/**
78	 * Body-center height above the ground for a mob kind. Mirrors the geometry
79	 * math in createMobModel so the headless simulation needs no Three.js meshes.
80	 */
81	export function mobHalfHeight(kind: MobKind): number {
82	  const bodyHeight = MOB_TEMPLATES[kind].modelArgs[5][1];
83	  const legHeight = Math.max(0.3, bodyHeight * 0.56);
84	  return Math.max(bodyHeight, legHeight) * 0.5 + 0.2;
85	}
86	
87	/** Builds the Three.js model for a mob kind (renderer side). */
88	export function createMobModelForKind(kind: MobKind) {
89	  return createMobModel(...MOB_TEMPLATES[kind].modelArgs);
90	}

19	  defense?: number;
20	  maxDurability?: number;
21	  /** Hunger points restored when eaten (food items only). */
22	  hunger?: number;
23	};

26	  id: string | null;
27	  label: string;
28	  kind: ItemKind | null;
29	  count: number;
30	  blockId?: BlockId;
31	  attack?: number;

44	  id: string;
45	  label: string;
46	  cost: Array<{ slotId: string; count: number }>;
47	  result: { slotId: string; count: number };
48	  /** Crafting station required; omitted means the basic crafting grid. */
49	  station?: "furnace";
50	};
51	
52	export type MobKind = "sheep" | "chicken" | "horse" | "zombie" | "skeleton" | "spider" | "boss";
53	
54	export type MobModel = {
55	  group: THREE.Group;
56	  legs: THREE.Mesh[];
57	  halfHeight: number;
58	  materials: THREE.Material[];
59	  geometries: THREE.BufferGeometry[];
60	};
61	
62	/** A persisted inventory/container slot: just enough to rebuild it on load. */
63	export type SavedSlot = { id: string | null; count: number; durability?: number };
64	
65	/** A block-entity: a placed chest's contents, keyed by its voxel index. */
66	export type SavedContainer = { index: number; slots: SavedSlot[] };
67	
68	/** Legacy save shape (40 inventory slots, 10-slot hotbar) — accepted and migrated on load. */
69	export type SaveDataV1 = {
70	  version: 1;
71	  seed: number;
72	  changes: Array<[number, number]>;
73	  inventoryCounts?: Record<string, number>;
74	  inventorySlots?: SavedSlot[];
75	  equippedArmor?: Partial<EquippedArmor>;
76	  selectedSlot: number;
77	  player: { x: number; y: number; z: number };
78	};
79	
80	/** v2 save shape: same fields as v1, reinterpreted for 36 slots / 9-slot hotbar. */
81	export type SaveDataV2 = Omit<SaveDataV1, "version"> & { version: 2 };
82	
83	/**
84	 * v3 save shape: v2 plus persisted time-of-day, player stats, and the bed
85	 * respawn point. All added fields are optional so the v2→v3 migration is a pure
86	 * version bump and pre-v3 saves load with sensible defaults.
87	 */
88	export type SaveDataV3 = Omit<SaveDataV2, "version"> & {
89	  version: 3;
90	  dayClock?: number;
91	  hearts?: number;
92	  hunger?: number;
93	  spawnPoint?: { x: number; y: number; z: number } | null;
94	};
95	
96	/**
97	 * v4 save shape: v3 plus chest contents as block-entities. The field is
98	 * optional so the v3→v4 migration is a pure version bump and pre-v4 saves load
99	 * with no containers.
100	 */
101	export type SaveDataV4 = Omit<SaveDataV3, "version"> & {
102	  version: 4;
103	  blockEntities?: SavedContainer[];
104	};
105	
106	/**
107	 * Current save shape (v5): v4 plus the set of dungeon loot-chest voxel indices
108	 * the player has already opened or broken. Dungeon chests are filled lazily on
109	 * first access and this set — not the chest's emptiness — is what prevents a
110	 * re-roll on reload (an emptied chest is dropped from `blockEntities`). The
111	 * field is optional so the v4→v5 migration is a pure version bump. Note
112	 * `SAVE_KEY` is bumped to v6 alongside this because the dungeon worldgen
113	 * changed the deterministic block-diff baseline.
114	 */
115	export type SaveData = Omit<SaveDataV4, "version"> & {
116	  version: 5;
117	  lootedChests?: number[];
118	  /** Generation preset; absent ⇒ "default" (pre-feature and legacy saves). Like `seed`, fixed for the world's life. */
119	  worldType?: WorldType;
120	};
121

1	import type { MobKind } from "@/lib/game/types";
2	
3	/**
4	 * A single loot entry: yields a uniformly random count in [min, max], gated by
5	 * an optional drop chance (default: always). Counts are inclusive on both ends.
6	 */
7	export type MobDrop = {
8	  itemId: string;
9	  min: number;
10	  max: number;
11	  /** 0..1 probability the entry drops at all; omitted means always. */
12	  chance?: number;
13	};
14	
15	/**
16	 * What each mob drops when it dies. Hostiles yield crafting/combat materials,
17	 * passives yield materials plus raw meat. Item ids must exist in ITEM_DEFS —
18	 * mobLoot.test.ts enforces that.
19	 */
20	export const MOB_DROPS: Record<MobKind, MobDrop[]> = {
21	  sheep: [
22	    { itemId: "wool", min: 1, max: 2 },
23	    { itemId: "raw_mutton", min: 1, max: 1 }
24	  ],
25	  chicken: [
26	    { itemId: "feather", min: 0, max: 2 },
27	    { itemId: "raw_chicken", min: 1, max: 1 }
28	  ],
29	  horse: [{ itemId: "leather", min: 1, max: 2 }],
30	  zombie: [{ itemId: "rotten_flesh", min: 1, max: 2 }],
31	  skeleton: [{ itemId: "bone", min: 1, max: 2 }],
32	  spider: [{ itemId: "string", min: 0, max: 2 }],
33	  boss: [
34	    { itemId: "dragon_heart", min: 1, max: 1 },
35	    { itemId: "diamond_ore", min: 2, max: 4 }
36	  ]
37	};
38	
39	/** Clamps an rng sample into [0, 1) so a pathological injected rng can't over-roll. */
40	const clampUnit = (v: number): number => Math.min(1 - Number.EPSILON, Math.max(0, v));
41	
42	/**
43	 * Rolls the drop table for a mob kind. Returns one entry per item that yielded a
44	 * positive count; the engine adds each to the inventory. `rng` is injectable so
45	 * tests get deterministic counts (0 → min, ~1 → max).
46	 */
47	export function rollMobDrops(kind: MobKind, rng: () => number): Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> {
48	  const drops: Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> = [];
49	  for (const entry of MOB_DROPS[kind]) {
50	    if (entry.chance !== undefined && clampUnit(rng()) >= entry.chance) continue;
51	    const span = entry.max - entry.min + 1;
52	    const count = entry.min + Math.floor(clampUnit(rng()) * span);
53	    if (count > 0) drops.push({ itemId: entry.itemId, count });
54	  }
55	  return drops;
56	}
57

196	export const ITEM_DEF_BY_ID: Record<string, ItemDef> = Object.fromEntries(ITEM_DEFS.map((item) => [item.id, item]));
197	
198	export function maxStackSizeForItem(itemId: string): number {
199	  return ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[itemId]?.maxDurability ? 1 : MAX_STACK_SIZE;
200	}
201	
202	export function createEmptySlot(): InventorySlot {
203	  return { id: null, label: "Empty", kind: null, count: 0 };
204	}
205	
206	export function createSlot(itemId: string, count: number): InventorySlot {
207	  const def = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[itemId];
208	  if (!def) return createEmptySlot();
209	  const slot: InventorySlot = { ...def, count: Math.min(maxStackSizeForItem(itemId), Math.max(0, Math.floor(count))) };
210	  if ((def.kind === "tool" || def.kind === "weapon" || def.kind === "armor") && def.maxDurability) {
211	    slot.maxDurability = def.maxDurability;
212	    slot.durability = def.maxDurability;
213	  }
214	  return slot;
215	}
216	
217	export function createInitialInventory(): InventorySlot[] {
218	  const slots: InventorySlot[] = Array.from({ length: INVENTORY_SLOTS }, () => createEmptySlot());
219	  const starter: Array<{ id: string; count: number }> = [
220	    { id: "grass", count: 64 },
221	    { id: "dirt", count: 64 },
222	    { id: "stone", count: 64 },
223	    { id: "wood", count: 64 },
224	    { id: "planks", count: 20 },
225	    { id: "cobble", count: 20 },
226	    { id: "sand", count: 20 },
227	    { id: "wood_pickaxe", count: 1 },
228	    { id: "knife", count: 1 }
229	  ];
230	  for (let i = 0; i < starter.length && i < slots.length; i += 1) slots[i] = createSlot(starter[i].id, starter[i].count);
231	  return slots;
232	}
233	
234	export const BLOCK_TO_SLOT: Partial<Record<BlockId, string>> = {
235	  [BlockId.Grass]: "grass",
236	  [BlockId.Dirt]: "dirt",
237	  [BlockId.Stone]: "stone",
238	  [BlockId.Wood]: "wood",
239	  [BlockId.Leaves]: "dirt",
240	  [BlockId.Planks]: "planks",
241	  [BlockId.Cobblestone]: "cobble",
242	  [BlockId.Sand]: "sand",
243	  [BlockId.Brick]: "brick",
244	  [BlockId.Glass]: "glass",
245	  [BlockId.SliverOre]: "sliver_ore",
246	  [BlockId.RubyOre]: "ruby_ore",
247	  [BlockId.GoldOre]: "gold_ore",
248	  [BlockId.SapphireOre]: "sapphire_ore",
249	  [BlockId.DiamondOre]: "diamond_ore",
250	  [BlockId.Snow]: "snow",
251	  [BlockId.Cactus]: "cactus",
252	  [BlockId.Bed]: "bed",
253	  [BlockId.Furnace]: "furnace",
254	  [BlockId.Chest]: "chest",
255	  [BlockId.MossyCobblestone]: "mossy_cobble",
256	  [BlockId.Torch]: "torch",
257	  [BlockId.DoorNorthLower]: "door",
258	  // Tilled soil reverts to dirt; immature wheat returns its seed.
259	  [BlockId.Farmland]: "dirt",
260	  [BlockId.WheatStage0]: "seeds",
261	  [BlockId.WheatStage1]: "seeds",
262	  [BlockId.WheatStage2]: "seeds"
263	};
264	
265	/**
266	 * Items a broken block yields. The default is its single `BLOCK_TO_SLOT` entry;
267	 * grass occasionally also drops a seed (the natural seed source), and mature
268	 * wheat drops wheat plus 1–2 seeds. `rng` is injectable for deterministic tests.
269	 */
270	export function rollBlockDrops(block: BlockId, rng: () => number): Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> {
271	  if (isDoorBlock(block)) return [{ itemId: "door", count: 1 }];
272	  const drops: Array<{ itemId: string; count: number }> = [];
273	  const base = BLOCK_TO_SLOT[block];
274	  if (base) drops.push({ itemId: base, count: 1 });
275	
276	  if (block === BlockId.Grass && rng() < GRASS_SEED_DROP_CHANCE) {
277	    drops.push({ itemId: "seeds", count: 1 });
278	  }
279	  if (block === BlockId.WheatStage3) {
280	    drops.push({ itemId: "wheat", count: 1 });
281	    drops.push({ itemId: "seeds", count: 1 + Math.floor(rng() * 2) });
282	  }
283	  return drops;
284	}
285

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { BlockId } from "@/lib/world";
3	import {
4	  BOSS_SUMMON_INTERVAL_SECONDS,
5	  HOSTILE_CAP,
6	  HOSTILE_SPAWN_BELOW_DAYLIGHT,
7	  HOSTILE_SPAWN_INTERVAL_SECONDS,
8	  RENDER_RADIUS,
9	  SPAWNER_ACTIVATION_RADIUS,
10	  SPAWNER_INTERVAL_SECONDS,
11	  SPAWNER_LOCAL_CAP
12	} from "@/lib/game/config";
13	import { MOB_TEMPLATES, mobHalfHeight } from "@/lib/game/mobs";
14	import { randomLandPointNear, type SurfaceYAtFn } from "@/lib/game/spawn";
15	import type { MobKind } from "@/lib/game/types";
16	import type { EmitGameEvent, GameState, MobState } from "../state";
17	
18	export type SpawnGroupArgs = {
19	  kind: MobKind;
20	  hostile: boolean;
21	  count: number;
22	  centerX: number;
23	  centerZ: number;
24	  radius: number;
25	};
26	
27	/** Adds one mob standing with its feet at (x, y, z) (y is the ground, not the body center). */
28	export function pushMob(state: GameState, kind: MobKind, hostile: boolean, x: number, y: number, z: number, rng: () => number): void {
29	  const template = MOB_TEMPLATES[kind];
30	  const halfHeight = mobHalfHeight(kind);
31	  const mob: MobState = {
32	    id: state.nextMobId,
33	    kind,
34	    hostile,
35	    hp: template.hp,
36	    position: new THREE.Vector3(x, y + halfHeight, z),
37	    direction: new THREE.Vector3(rng() - 0.5, 0, rng() - 0.5).normalize(),
38	    yaw: 0,
39	    turnTimer: 1 + rng() * 3,
40	    speed: template.speed,
41	    moveSpeed: template.speed,
42	    detectRange: template.detectRange,
43	    attackDamage: template.attackDamage,
44	    attackCooldown: template.attackCooldown,
45	    attackTimer: rng(),
46	    halfHeight,
47	    bobSeed: rng() * 10,
48	    fedTimer: 0,
49	    ageTimer: 0
50	  };
51	  state.nextMobId += 1;
52	  state.mobs.push(mob);
53	}
54	
55	/**
56	 * Spawns the single endgame boss with its feet at (x, y, z), bypassing the
57	 * hostile-cap directors (the totem summon is always allowed). Seeds its minion
58	 * timer so the first summon waits a full interval.
59	 */
60	export function spawnBoss(state: GameState, x: number, y: number, z: number, rng: () => number): void {
61	  pushMob(state, "boss", true, x, y, z, rng);
62	  state.mobs[state.mobs.length - 1].summonTimer = BOSS_SUMMON_INTERVAL_SECONDS;
63	}
64	
65	export function spawnMobGroup(state: GameState, args: SpawnGroupArgs, rng: () => number, surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn): void {
66	  for (let i = 0; i < args.count; i += 1) {
67	    const spawnPos = randomLandPointNear(state.world, surfaceYAt, args.centerX, args.centerZ, args.radius, rng);
68	    pushMob(state, args.kind, args.hostile, spawnPos.x, spawnPos.y, spawnPos.z, rng);
69	  }
70	}
71	
72	/**
73	 * The day-one population around the player's spawn point. Passives scatter
74	 * over a wider ring than hostiles so the spawn area doesn't feel like a
75	 * petting zoo; hostiles stay closer (the dawn-aggro behavior tests document).
76	 */
77	export function spawnInitialMobs(state: GameState, rng: () => number, surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn): void {
78	  const centerX = state.player.position.x;
79	  const centerZ = state.player.position.z;
80	  const passiveRadius = RENDER_RADIUS * 1.2;
81	  const hostileRadius = RENDER_RADIUS * 0.7;
82	  const groups: Array<[MobKind, boolean, number, number]> = [
83	    ["sheep", false, 6, passiveRadius],
84	    ["chicken", false, 5, passiveRadius],
85	    ["horse", false, 3, passiveRadius],
86	    ["zombie", true, 8, hostileRadius],
87	    ["skeleton", true, 6, hostileRadius],
88	    ["spider", true, 6, hostileRadius]
89	  ];
90	  for (const [kind, hostile, count, radius] of groups) {
91	    spawnMobGroup(state, { kind, hostile, count, centerX, centerZ, radius }, rng, surfaceYAt);
92	  }
93	}
94	
95	/** Trickles hostile mobs in around the player at night, up to the cap. */
96	export function tickHostileSpawnDirector(state: GameState, dt: number, rng: () => number, surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn): void {
97	  state.timers.hostileSpawnTimer += dt;
98	  if (state.daylight >= HOSTILE_SPAWN_BELOW_DAYLIGHT || state.timers.hostileSpawnTimer < HOSTILE_SPAWN_INTERVAL_SECONDS) return;
99	  state.timers.hostileSpawnTimer = 0;
100	
101	  const livingHostiles = state.mobs.filter((mob) => mob.hostile).length;
102	  if (livingHostiles >= HOSTILE_CAP) return;
103	
104	  const spawnKinds: Array<"zombie" | "skeleton" | "spider"> = ["zombie", "skeleton", "spider"];
105	  const kind = spawnKinds[Math.floor(rng() * spawnKinds.length)];
106	  spawnMobGroup(
107	    state,
108	    {
109	      kind,
110	      hostile: true,
111	      count: 1 + (rng() > 0.7 ? 1 : 0),
112	      centerX: state.player.position.x,
113	      centerZ: state.player.position.z,
114	      radius: Math.max(26, RENDER_RADIUS * 0.85)
115	    },
116	    rng,
117	    surfaceYAt
118	  );
119	}
120	
121	const SPAWNER_KINDS: ReadonlyArray<"zombie" | "skeleton" | "spider"> = ["zombie", "skeleton", "spider"];
122	
123	/**
124	 * Drips hostiles from dungeon spawners. On each interval, every intact spawner
125	 * with the player inside its activation radius spawns one hostile on the room
126	 * floor (feet at the spawner's own y, not the surface), bounded by a local
127	 * cluster cap and the shared global HOSTILE_CAP. Time-independent — dungeons
128	 * are dark. Spawner positions come from the session-derived index set, so
129	 * mining out a spawner block stops it (the world.get check below).
130	 */
131	export function tickSpawnerDirector(state: GameState, dt: number, rng: () => number, emit: EmitGameEvent): void {
132	  if (state.dungeonSpawnerIndices.size === 0) return;
133	  state.timers.spawnerTimer += dt;
134	  if (state.timers.spawnerTimer < SPAWNER_INTERVAL_SECONDS) return;
135	  state.timers.spawnerTimer = 0;
136	
137	  const countHostiles = () => state.mobs.reduce((acc, mob) => acc + (mob.hostile ? 1 : 0), 0);
138	  if (countHostiles() >= HOSTILE_CAP) return;
139	
140	  const { player, world } = state;
141	  const layer = world.sizeX * world.sizeZ;
142	  for (const idx of state.dungeonSpawnerIndices) {
143	    const sy = Math.floor(idx / layer);
144	    const rem = idx - sy * layer;
145	    const sz = Math.floor(rem / world.sizeX);
146	    const sx = rem - sz * world.sizeX;
147	    if (world.get(sx, sy, sz) !== BlockId.Spawner) continue; // mined out → inert
148	    if (Math.hypot(player.position.x - (sx + 0.5), player.position.y - (sy + 0.5), player.position.z - (sz + 0.5)) > SPAWNER_ACTIVATION_RADIUS) continue;
149	    if (countHostiles() >= HOSTILE_CAP) break;
150	
151	    const nearby = state.mobs.reduce(
152	      (acc, mob) => acc + (mob.hostile && Math.hypot(mob.position.x - (sx + 0.5), mob.position.z - (sz + 0.5)) <= SPAWNER_ACTIVATION_RADIUS ? 1 : 0),
153	      0
154	    );
155	    if (nearby >= SPAWNER_LOCAL_CAP) continue;
156	
157	    const kind = SPAWNER_KINDS[Math.floor(rng() * SPAWNER_KINDS.length)];
158	    const jx = sx + 0.5 + (rng() * 2 - 1) * 1.2;
159	    const jz = sz + 0.5 + (rng() * 2 - 1) * 1.2;
160	    pushMob(state, kind, true, jx, sy, jz, rng);
161	    emit({ type: "mobSpawned", kind, x: sx + 0.5, y: sy + 0.5, z: sz + 0.5 });
162	  }
163	}
164

1	import type { MobKind } from "@/lib/game/types";
2	
3	/** Mobs farther than this are silent. */
4	export const MOB_EARSHOT = 24;
5	
6	/** Seconds between idle calls per kind (min..max, uniform). */
7	const CALL_INTERVALS: Record<MobKind, readonly [number, number]> = {
8	  sheep: [6, 14],
9	  chicken: [5, 12],
10	  horse: [8, 18],
11	  zombie: [4, 9],
12	  skeleton: [5, 11],
13	  spider: [4, 10],
14	  boss: [6, 12]
15	};
16	
17	/** Structural subset of the engine's MobState — state.mobs passes directly. */
18	export type AmbientMob = {
19	  id: number;
20	  kind: MobKind;
21	  position: { x: number; z: number };
22	};
23	
24	export type AmbientCall = {
25	  kind: MobKind;
26	  /** Distance falloff 0..1. */
27	  gain: number;
28	  /** Stereo position -1..1 relative to the player's look direction. */
29	  pan: number;
30	};
31	
32	export type MobAmbienceScheduler = {
33	  /** Returns calls due this frame. The array is reused — consume immediately. */
34	  tick(dt: number, mobs: readonly AmbientMob[], playerX: number, playerZ: number, playerYaw: number): readonly AmbientCall[];
35	};
36	
37	export function createMobAmbienceScheduler(rng: () => number = Math.random): MobAmbienceScheduler {
38	  const timers = new Map<number, number>();
39	  const calls: AmbientCall[] = [];
40	
41	  const nextInterval = (kind: MobKind): number => {
42	    const [min, max] = CALL_INTERVALS[kind];
43	    return min + rng() * (max - min);
44	  };
45	
46	  return {
47	    tick(dt, mobs, playerX, playerZ, playerYaw) {
48	      calls.length = 0;
49	
50	      // Despawned mobs leave stale timers behind; sweep once the map outgrows
51	      // the population instead of allocating an id set every frame.
52	      if (timers.size > mobs.length + 8) {
53	        const alive = new Set(mobs.map((mob) => mob.id));
54	        for (const id of timers.keys()) if (!alive.has(id)) timers.delete(id);
55	      }
56	
57	      const rightX = Math.cos(playerYaw);
58	      const rightZ = -Math.sin(playerYaw);
59	
60	      for (const mob of mobs) {
61	        const dx = mob.position.x - playerX;
62	        const dz = mob.position.z - playerZ;
63	        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
64	        if (dist > MOB_EARSHOT) continue;
65	
66	        const timer = timers.get(mob.id);
67	        if (timer === undefined) {
68	          // First sighting: start a full randomized interval so a spawn wave
69	          // doesn't open with a chorus.
70	          timers.set(mob.id, nextInterval(mob.kind));
71	          continue;
72	        }
73	        const remaining = timer - dt;
74	        if (remaining > 0) {
75	          timers.set(mob.id, remaining);
76	          continue;
77	        }
78	        timers.set(mob.id, nextInterval(mob.kind));
79	
80	        const falloff = 1 - dist / MOB_EARSHOT;
81	        const pan = dist > 0.001 ? Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, (dx * rightX + dz * rightZ) / dist)) : 0;
82	        calls.push({ kind: mob.kind, gain: falloff * falloff, pan });
83	      }
84	      return calls;
85	    }
86	  };
87	}
88	
```

106	const FEED_ITEMS: Partial<Record<MobKind, string>> = {
107	  sheep: "wheat",
108	  horse: "wheat",
109	  chicken: "seeds"
110	};

112	/**
113	 * Right-click an adult animal with its feed item to put it "in love" (toward
114	 * breeding). First in the right-click precedence so feeding wins over placing or
115	 * tilling when an animal is in the crosshair. Returns true when an animal was fed.
116	 */
117	export function tryFeedAimedMob(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent): boolean {
118	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
119	  if (!slot?.id || slot.count <= 0) return false;
120	  const index = findAimedMobIndex(state);
121	  if (index < 0) return false;
122	  const mob = state.mobs[index];
123	  if (mob.hostile || FEED_ITEMS[mob.kind] !== slot.id) return false;
124	  if (mob.ageTimer > 0 || mob.fedTimer > 0) return false; // babies and already-in-love animals decline
125	
126	  state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, -1, state.selectedSlot) ?? state.inventory;
127	  mob.fedTimer = BREED_FED_WINDOW_SECONDS;
128	  emit({ type: "mobFed", kind: mob.kind });
129	  return true;
130	}
131	
132	/** Opens the inventory in furnace mode so its smelting recipes unlock. */
133	function interactFurnace(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent): boolean {
134	  state.inventoryOpen = true;
135	  state.craftingStation = "furnace";
136	  emit({ type: "openedStation", station: "furnace" });
137	  return true;
138	}
139	
140	/** Sleep in a bed: only at night, only when no hostile is near. Sets the respawn point. */
141	function interactBed(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent, x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean {
142	  if (state.daylight >= SLEEP_ALLOWED_BELOW_DAYLIGHT) {
143	    emit({ type: "sleepDenied", reason: "daylight" });
144	    return true;
145	  }
146	  for (const mob of state.mobs) {
147	    if (mob.hostile && mob.position.distanceTo(state.player.position) <= SLEEP_HOSTILE_RADIUS) {
148	      emit({ type: "sleepDenied", reason: "hostiles" });
149	      return true;
150	    }
151	  }
152	
153	  state.spawnPoint = { x, y, z };
154	  state.sleepTimer = SLEEP_FADE_SECONDS;

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { BABY_GROW_SECONDS, BABY_SCALE, BREED_CHECK_INTERVAL_SECONDS, BREED_PARTNER_RADIUS, PASSIVE_CAP } from "@/lib/game/config";
3	import { MOB_TEMPLATES, mobHalfHeight } from "@/lib/game/mobs";
4	import type { SurfaceYAtFn } from "@/lib/game/spawn";
5	import type { EmitGameEvent, GameState, MobState } from "../state";
6	
7	/**
8	 * Animal breeding. Each tick ages babies up and counts down the "in love"
9	 * window; on an interval it pairs two fed adults of the same kind that are close
10	 * together into a baby, bounded by PASSIVE_CAP. Mobs are never persisted, so
11	 * breeding state is session-only by the engine's existing design.
12	 */
13	export function tickBreeding(state: GameState, dt: number, rng: () => number, surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn, emit: EmitGameEvent): void {
14	  const { mobs } = state;
15	
16	  // Per-tick: tick down love + maturity timers; a matured baby regains full size.
17	  for (const mob of mobs) {
18	    if (mob.fedTimer > 0) mob.fedTimer = Math.max(0, mob.fedTimer - dt);
19	    if (mob.ageTimer > 0) {
20	      mob.ageTimer = Math.max(0, mob.ageTimer - dt);
21	      if (mob.ageTimer === 0) mob.halfHeight = mobHalfHeight(mob.kind);
22	    }
23	  }
24	
25	  state.timers.breedTimer += dt;
26	  if (state.timers.breedTimer < BREED_CHECK_INTERVAL_SECONDS) return;
27	  state.timers.breedTimer = 0;
28	
29	  let passiveCount = mobs.reduce((acc, mob) => acc + (mob.hostile ? 0 : 1), 0);
30	  const radiusSq = BREED_PARTNER_RADIUS * BREED_PARTNER_RADIUS;
31	
32	  for (let i = 0; i < mobs.length; i += 1) {
33	    if (passiveCount >= PASSIVE_CAP) break;
34	    const a = mobs[i];
35	    if (a.hostile || a.fedTimer <= 0 || a.ageTimer > 0) continue;
36	    for (let j = i + 1; j < mobs.length; j += 1) {
37	      const b = mobs[j];
38	      if (b.hostile || b.kind !== a.kind || b.fedTimer <= 0 || b.ageTimer > 0) continue;
39	      if (a.position.distanceToSquared(b.position) > radiusSq) continue;
40	
41	      spawnBaby(state, a, rng, surfaceYAt);
42	      passiveCount += 1;
43	      a.fedTimer = 0;
44	      b.fedTimer = 0;
45	      emit({ type: "mobBred", kind: a.kind });
46	      break; // a has bred; move to the next animal
47	    }
48	  }
49	}
50	
51	/** Spawns a juvenile of the parent's kind at the midpoint, scaled down until grown. */
52	function spawnBaby(state: GameState, parent: MobState, rng: () => number, surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn): void {
53	  const template = MOB_TEMPLATES[parent.kind];
54	  const halfHeight = mobHalfHeight(parent.kind) * BABY_SCALE;
55	  const x = parent.position.x;
56	  const z = parent.position.z;
57	  const baby: MobState = {
58	    id: state.nextMobId,
59	    kind: parent.kind,
60	    hostile: false,
61	    hp: template.hp,
62	    position: new THREE.Vector3(x, surfaceYAt(x, z) + halfHeight, z),
63	    direction: new THREE.Vector3(rng() - 0.5, 0, rng() - 0.5).normalize(),
64	    yaw: 0,
65	    turnTimer: 1 + rng() * 3,
66	    speed: template.speed,
67	    moveSpeed: template.speed,
68	    detectRange: template.detectRange,
69	    attackDamage: template.attackDamage,
70	    attackCooldown: template.attackCooldown,
71	    attackTimer: rng(),
72	    halfHeight,
73	    bobSeed: rng() * 10,
74	    fedTimer: 0,
75	    ageTimer: BABY_GROW_SECONDS
76	  };
77	  state.nextMobId += 1;
78	  state.mobs.push(baby);
79	}
80

=== mobLoot.test.ts ===
import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
import { ITEM_DEF_BY_ID } from "@/lib/game/items";
import { MOB_DROPS, rollMobDrops } from "@/lib/game/mobLoot";
import type { MobKind } from "@/lib/game/types";

// Derived from the table so new mob kinds are covered automatically.
const ALL_KINDS = Object.keys(MOB_DROPS) as MobKind[];

describe("mob drop tables", () => {
  test("every drop references an existing item", () => {
    for (const kind of ALL_KINDS) {
      for (const entry of MOB_DROPS[kind]) {
        expect(ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[entry.itemId]).toBeDefined();
        expect(entry.min).toBeLessThanOrEqual(entry.max);
        expect(entry.min).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
      }
    }
  });

  test("rng at 0 yields the minimum count of each guaranteed entry", () => {
    // rng() === 0 takes any chance entry and floors the count to its minimum.
    const drops = rollMobDrops("sheep", () => 0);
    const byId = new Map(drops.map((d) => [d.itemId, d.count]));
    expect(byId.get("wool")).toBe(1); // min 1
    expect(byId.get("raw_mutton")).toBe(1); // min 1
  });

  test("rng near 1 yields the maximum count", () => {
    const rng = () => 0.999;
    const drops = rollMobDrops("sheep", rng);
    const byId = new Map(drops.map((d) => [d.itemId, d.count]));
    expect(byId.get("wool")).toBe(2); // max 2
    expect(byId.get("raw_mutton")).toBe(1);
  });

  test("a zero-minimum entry can drop nothing at the low roll", () => {
    // chicken feather is min 0; at rng 0 the count floors to 0 and is omitted.
    const drops = rollMobDrops("chicken", () => 0);
    expect(drops.some((d) => d.itemId === "feather")).toBe(false);
    expect(drops.some((d) => d.itemId === "raw_chicken")).toBe(true);
  });

  test("every kind has at least one drop entry", () => {
    for (const kind of ALL_KINDS) expect(MOB_DROPS[kind].length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});
```

=== recipes.test.ts ===
import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
import { createEmptySlot, createSlot, ITEM_DEF_BY_ID } from "@/lib/game/items";
import { canCraft, countsById, craft } from "@/lib/game/inventory";
import { RECIPES } from "@/lib/game/recipes";
import type { InventorySlot } from "@/lib/game/types";

function inventory(items: Array<[string, number]>): InventorySlot[] {
  const slots = Array.from({ length: 12 }, () => createEmptySlot());
  items.forEach(([id, count], i) => {
    slots[i] = createSlot(id, count);
  });
  return slots;
}

const recipe = (id: string) => {
  const found = RECIPES.find((r) => r.id === id);
  if (!found) throw new Error(`recipe ${id} not found`);
  return found;
};

describe("recipe integrity", () => {
  test("every recipe references only real item ids", () => {
    for (const r of RECIPES) {
      for (const cost of r.cost) expect(ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[cost.slotId], `${r.id} cost ${cost.slotId}`).toBeDefined();
      expect(ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[r.result.slotId], `${r.id} result ${r.result.slotId}`).toBeDefined();
    }
  });
});

describe("ranged recipes", () => {
  test("a bow is craftable from wood + string", () => {
    const slots = inventory([
      ["wood", 3],
      ["string", 3]
    ]);
    expect(canCraft(slots, recipe("bow"))).toBe(true);
    const next = craft(slots, recipe("bow"));
    expect(next).not.toBeNull();
    expect(countsById(next!).get("bow")).toBe(1);
  });

  test("arrows craft four at a time from stone + wood + feather", () => {
    const slots = inventory([
      ["stone", 1],
      ["wood", 1],
      ["feather", 1]
    ]);
    expect(canCraft(slots, recipe("arrow"))).toBe(true);
    const next = craft(slots, recipe("arrow"));
    expect(next).not.toBeNull();
    expect(countsById(next!).get("arrow")).toBe(4);
  });

  test("a bow cannot be crafted without enough string", () => {
    const slots = inventory([
      ["wood", 3],
      ["string", 2]
    ]);
    expect(canCraft(slots, recipe("bow"))).toBe(false);
  });
});

=== mobVisuals.test.ts ===
import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
import * as THREE from "three";
import { createMobVisuals } from "@/lib/game/render/mobVisuals";
import { mobHalfHeight } from "@/lib/game/mobs";
import type { MobState } from "@/lib/game/engine/state";

function makeMob(id: number, overrides: Partial<MobState> = {}): MobState {
  return {
    id,
    kind: "sheep",
    hostile: false,
    hp: 10,
    position: new THREE.Vector3(10, 50, 10),
    direction: new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0),
    yaw: 0.5,
    turnTimer: 1,
    speed: 0.9,
    moveSpeed: 0.9,
    detectRange: 0,
    attackDamage: 0,
    attackCooldown: 0,
    attackTimer: 0,
    halfHeight: mobHalfHeight("sheep"),
    bobSeed: 1,
    fedTimer: 0,
    ageTimer: 0,
    ...overrides
  };
}

describe("mobVisuals", () => {
  test("creates one model per mob and removes models for despawned mobs", () => {
    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    const visuals = createMobVisuals(scene);
    const baseChildren = scene.children.length;

    visuals.sync([makeMob(1), makeMob(2, { kind: "zombie" })], 0);
    expect(scene.children.length).toBe(baseChildren + 2);

    visuals.sync([makeMob(2, { kind: "zombie" })], 0); // mob 1 despawned
    expect(scene.children.length).toBe(baseChildren + 1);

    visuals.dispose();
    expect(scene.children.length).toBe(baseChildren);
  });

  test("reuses the same model across frames for the same mob id", () => {
    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    const visuals = createMobVisuals(scene);

    visuals.sync([makeMob(7)], 0);
    const group = scene.children[scene.children.length - 1];
    visuals.sync([makeMob(7)], 100);
    expect(scene.children[scene.children.length - 1]).toBe(group);
    visuals.dispose();
  });

  test("applies position, yaw, and a time-varying bob offset", () => {
    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    const visuals = createMobVisuals(scene);
    const mob = makeMob(1, { yaw: 1.25 });

    visuals.sync([mob], 0);
    const group = scene.children[scene.children.length - 1];
    expect(group.position.x).toBe(10);
    expect(group.position.z).toBe(10);
    expect(group.rotation.y).toBe(1.25);
    expect(Math.abs(group.position.y - 50)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.04); // bob amplitude

    const y1 = group.position.y;
    visuals.sync([mob], 100); // quarter of the bob period later
    expect(group.position.y).not.toBe(y1);
    visuals.dispose();
  });
});

=== breeding.test.ts (head) ===
import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
import * as THREE from "three";
import { BABY_SCALE, BREED_CHECK_INTERVAL_SECONDS, BREED_FED_WINDOW_SECONDS, PASSIVE_CAP } from "@/lib/game/config";
import { mobHalfHeight } from "@/lib/game/mobs";
import { createTimers, type GameEvent, type GameState, type MobState } from "@/lib/game/engine/state";
import { tickBreeding } from "@/lib/game/engine/systems/breeding";

function makeSheep(id: number, x: number, z: number, fedTimer = 0, ageTimer = 0): MobState {
  return {
    id,
    kind: "sheep",
    hostile: false,
    hp: 10,
    position: new THREE.Vector3(x, 50, z),
    direction: new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0),
    yaw: 0,
    turnTimer: 1,
    speed: 0.9,
    moveSpeed: 0.9,
    detectRange: 0,
    attackDamage: 0,
    attackCooldown: 0,
    attackTimer: 0,
    halfHeight: mobHalfHeight("sheep"),
    bobSeed: 0,
    fedTimer,
    ageTimer
  };
}

function makeState(mobs: MobState[]): GameState {
  return { mobs, nextMobId: 1000, timers: createTimers() } as unknown as GameState;
}

const surfaceYAt = () => 49;
const rng = () => 0.7;

describe("breeding", () => {
  test("two fed adults nearby produce one baby and clear their love timers", () => {
    const a = makeSheep(1, 0, 0, BREED_FED_WINDOW_SECONDS);
    const b = makeSheep(2, 1, 0, BREED_FED_WINDOW_SECONDS); // distance 1 < radius 3
    const state = makeState([a, b]);
    const events: GameEvent[] = [];
    tickBreeding(state, BREED_CHECK_INTERVAL_SECONDS, rng, surfaceYAt, (e) => events.push(e));

    expect(state.mobs).toHaveLength(3);
    const baby = state.mobs[2];
    expect(baby.kind).toBe("sheep");
    expect(baby.ageTimer).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(baby.halfHeight).toBeCloseTo(mobHalfHeight("sheep") * BABY_SCALE, 5);
```

104	## Blocks
105	
106	**32 block types** (plus air). Hardness is relative break time — higher is slower.
107	"Mine with" is the minimum tool needed; blocks with no requirement break with bare
108	hands or any tool. Bedrock, water, and lava cannot be broken.
109	
110	| Block             | Hardness | Mine with       | Notes                                                                                                         |
111	| ----------------- | -------- | --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
112	| Grass             | 2        | any             | Drops dirt; ~20% chance to also drop a seed                                                                   |
113	| Dirt              | 2        | any             | —                                                                                                             |
114	| Sand              | 2        | any             | Common on beaches and in deserts                                                                              |
115	| Snow              | 2        | any             | Mountain peaks                                                                                                |
116	| Leaves            | 2        | any             | From trees; drops dirt                                                                                        |
117	| Cactus            | 2        | any             | Desert decoration                                                                                             |
118	| Glass             | 2        | any             | Crafted from sand; clear when placed                                                                          |
119	| Wood              | 3        | any             | Tree trunks                                                                                                   |
120	| Planks            | 3        | any             | Crafted from wood                                                                                             |
121	| Stone             | 5        | Wood Pickaxe    | Drops the stone item                                                                                          |
122	| Cobblestone       | 5        | Wood Pickaxe    | Drops cobble (crafting staple)                                                                                |
123	| Brick             | 5        | Wood Pickaxe    | Crafted; also found in houses                                                                                 |
124	| Furnace           | 5        | Wood Pickaxe    | **Interactive** — opens smelting recipes                                                                      |
125	| Chest             | 3        | any             | **Interactive** — 27-slot storage; breaking it spills the contents into your inventory (refused if it's full) |
126	| Wood Door         | 3        | any             | **Interactive** — thin 1×2 panel; right-click to open/close; mobs cannot operate it                           |
127	| Torch             | 1        | any             | Place it to light the dark; emits block light 14. Crafted 4-at-a-time from 1 wood                             |
128	| Lava              | —        | (unbreakable)   | Glows in the deepest caves; **burns on contact** (3 hearts / 0.5 s, armor-bypassing). Worldgen-only, no item  |
129	| Mossy Cobblestone | 5        | Wood Pickaxe    | Dungeon walls; mineable into a `mossy_cobble` item (found-only, no recipe)                                    |
130	| Spawner           | 30       | (unbreakable\*) | Dungeon-only; drips hostiles when you're near. Very hard and drops nothing — mining it out just stops it      |
131	| Sliver Ore        | 7        | Stone Pickaxe   | —                                                                                                             |
132	| Ruby Ore          | 9        | Sliver Pickaxe  | —                                                                                                             |
133	| Gold Ore          | 11       | Sliver Pickaxe  | —                                                                                                             |
134	| Sapphire Ore      | 12       | Ruby Pickaxe    | —                                                                                                             |
135	| Diamond Ore       | 14       | Ruby Pickaxe    | Deepest, rarest ore                                                                                           |
136	| Bed               | 2        | any             | **Interactive** — sleep & set spawn                                                                           |

138	| Wheat (stage 0–2) | 1        | any             | Immature crop; drops its seed                                                                                 |
139	| Wheat (stage 3)   | 1        | any             | Mature crop; drops wheat + 1–2 seeds                                                                          |
140	| Bedrock           | —        | unbreakable     | World floor and border                                                                                        |
141	| Water             | —        | —               | Liquid; place blocks into it to replace cells; 60 s continuous immersion starts 1.5-heart damage each second  |

144	
145	**6 mob kinds.** Passive animals flee but never attack and can be bred; hostiles

157	
158	Skeletons are now **ranged** — they kite and fire arrows instead of meleeing.
159	Spiders are hostile only in the dark (daylight below 0.42); in twilight and day
160	they behave like passives. **Breeding:** feed wheat to a sheep or horse, or seeds
161	to a chicken; babies grow up in ~90 s. Passive population is capped at 24, hostiles
162	at 16.

242	| -------------- | --------------- | ------------------ |
243	| Cooked Chicken | 8               | Smelt raw chicken  |
244	| Cooked Mutton  | 8               | Smelt raw mutton   |

249	| Rotten Flesh   | 2               | Zombie drop        |
250	
251	### Materials
252

254	**Bone**, **Leather**, **String** (mob drops), and **Wheat Seeds** / **Wheat**

246	## Mobs and breeding
247	
248	Six creatures roam the world:
249	
250	- **Passive** — **sheep**, **chicken**, **horse**. They wander, flee when you get
251	  close, and never attack. They drop materials and raw meat when killed.
252	- **Hostile** — **zombie**, **skeleton**, **spider**. They hunt you at night,
253	  chase within their detection range, and attack when they have line of sight.
254	  **Skeletons are archers** — they keep their distance and fire arrows, so close
255	  in or take cover. Spiders are only hostile in the dark.
256	
257	Full stats and drop tables are in the [bestiary](reference.md#mobs). The summoned
258	**boss** is covered under [Endgame](#endgame-the-boss).
259	
260	**Breeding** makes loot renewable. Right-click an adult **sheep or horse with
261	wheat**, or a **chicken with seeds**, to put it "in love" for **30 seconds**. Two
262	in-love adults of the same kind standing within **3 blocks** produce a **baby**,
263	which follows its parents, drops nothing while young, and grows to full size after
264	**~90 seconds**. The passive population is capped (and feeding costs crops), so it
265	stays under control.
266	
267	## Endgame: the boss
```

1	# Changelog
2	
3	All notable changes to this project are documented in this file.
4	
5	## [Unreleased]
6	
7	### Added
8	
9	- **World types**: when creating a world you now pick a generation preset, not just a seed — **Default**, **Superflat** (level builder's canvas), **Amplified** (towering, dramatic relief), or **Islands** (scattered land in a raised sea). Each generates genuinely different terrain over the same biome map; **zero new assets** (the picker reuses the menu chrome)
10	  - `generateWorld(world, worldType)` forks on the type before any block write via `terrainConfigFor` (`lib/world/generation.ts`), varying only sea level and per-biome surface height; caves, ores, lava, trees, structures, and dungeons are unchanged. The **Default path is byte-identical** to before — existing worlds and the determinism hashes are untouched, so **no `WORLDGEN_VERSION` bump**. Each new type is pinned by its own SHA-256 baseline plus structural probes (flat is level, amplified spans a wider band, islands floods more of the map while still leaving a dry spawn)
11	  - The chosen type is stored on `WorldMeta` and in the `SaveData` blob (like `seed`), so a world always regenerates with the type its block-diffs were recorded against. `WorldType` lives in `lib/world/worldTypes.ts`; the schema stays at version 5 (the field is optional, absent ⇒ default)
12	- **Multiple worlds & player profiles**: the game now opens to a menu instead of dropping straight into one fixed world. A **profile** (name + skin) owns a list of named **worlds**, like user accounts on a shared console — pick a profile, then pick or create a world to play. All client-side in localStorage; **zero new assets** (the menus reuse the existing Minecraft-style chrome and generated skin portraits)
```

2	import { BlockId } from "@/lib/world";

5	
6	function opaquePixelCount(pixels: Uint8ClampedArray): number {
7	  let count = 0;
8	  for (let i = 3; i < pixels.length; i += 4) if (pixels[i] > 0) count += 1;
9	  return count;
10	}
11	
12	describe("renderSpritePixels", () => {
13	  // Integrity-style: every registered item must produce a real sprite, so a
14	  // new ITEM_DEFS entry without icon coverage fails here instead of falling
15	  // back to the magenta checker silently.
16	  test("every item def yields a non-empty 16x16 sprite", () => {
17	    const placeholder = renderSpritePixels("no_such_item");
18	    for (const def of ITEM_DEFS) {
19	      const pixels = renderSpritePixels(def.id);
20	      expect(pixels.length).toBe(SPRITE_SIZE * SPRITE_SIZE * 4);
21	      expect(opaquePixelCount(pixels)).toBeGreaterThan(20);
22	      expect(pixels).not.toEqual(placeholder); // no item may fall back to the checker
23	    }
24	  });
25	
26	  test("is deterministic", () => {
27	    expect(renderSpritePixels("diamond_sword")).toEqual(renderSpritePixels("diamond_sword"));
28	    expect(paintIsoBlock(BlockId.Grass)).toEqual(paintIsoBlock(BlockId.Grass));

33	    expect(renderSpritePixels("wood_sword")).not.toEqual(renderSpritePixels("gold_sword"));
34	    expect(renderSpritePixels("wood_spear")).not.toEqual(renderSpritePixels("diamond_spear"));
35	  });
36

38	    expect(renderSpritePixels("stone_spear")).not.toEqual(renderSpritePixels("stone_sword"));
39	  });

41	  test("the knife silhouette is distinct from every sword", () => {
42	    const knife = renderSpritePixels("knife");

51	        return count;

90	    expect(pixels[3]).toBe(0); // top-left corner alpha
91	    expect(pixels[(SPRITE_SIZE - 1) * 4 + 3]).toBe(0); // top-right corner alpha
92	  });
93	});
94	
95	describe("paintGrid", () => {
96	  test("maps palette characters and leaves dots transparent", () => {
97	    const pixels = paintGrid(["xx", ".x"], { x: [10, 20, 30] });
98	    expect([pixels[0], pixels[1], pixels[2], pixels[3]]).toEqual([10, 20, 30, 255]);
99	    expect(pixels[(SPRITE_SIZE + 0) * 4 + 3]).toBe(0); // row 1 col 0 is "."
100	  });
101	});

671	function blockRgb(blockId: BlockId): Rgb {
672	  const float = BLOCK_COLORS[blockId] ?? [0.6, 0.6, 0.6];

Both raw meats smelt\n  to a cooked form (hunger 8) at a furnace, and both breed through the\n  existing generic path (feed a cow wheat, a pig seeds).\n- Procedural box models reuse createMobModel; new moo/grunt ambient calls and\n  benign attack-sound entries keep the exhaustive Record<MobKind> sound tables\n  complete. New meat sprites reuse the shared raw-meat grid with new palettes.\n\nMobs and breeding are session-only and the new foods save by string id, so\nthere is no save-format or worldgen change. Spawn counts, recipe, breeding,\nand loot tests updated; reference/manual docs and CHANGELOG cover the additions.\nEOF\ngit log --oneline -1",

41	  canvas.width = width;

47	  const drawTile = (block: number, face: "top" | "side" | "bottom") => {
48	    const tile = tileIndexFor(block, face);
49	    const col = tile % ATLAS_COLUMNS;
50	    const row = Math.floor(tile / ATLAS_COLUMNS);
51	    const ox = col * ATLAS_TILE_SIZE;
52	    const oy = row * ATLAS_TILE_SIZE;
53	
54	    const baseBlockColor = BLOCK_COLORS[block] ?? [1, 0, 1];
55	    let base = baseBlockColor;
56	    if (face === "top") base = tone(base, 1.08);
57	    if (face === "bottom") base = tone(base, 0.96);
58	    if (block === BlockId.Grass && face === "bottom") base = BLOCK_COLORS[BlockId.Dirt];
59	
60	    for (let y = 0; y < ATLAS_TILE_SIZE; y += 1) {
61	      for (let x = 0; x < ATLAS_TILE_SIZE; x += 1) {
62	        const h = Math.sin((x + block * 13 + (face === "top" ? 7 : face === "side" ? 17 : 29)) * 12.1 + (y + block * 19) * 7.7) * 43758.5453;
63	        const n = h - Math.floor(h);
64	        let c = tone(base, 0.92 + n * 0.22);
65	
66	        if (block === BlockId.Grass && face === "side" && y < 4) c = tone(BLOCK_COLORS[BlockId.Grass], 0.95 + n * 0.15);
67	        if ((block === BlockId.Stone || block === BlockId.Cobblestone || block === BlockId.Bedrock) && n > 0.8) c = tone(base, 1.18);
68	        if ((block === BlockId.Wood || block === BlockId.Planks) && (x + y) % 4 === 0) c = tone(base, 0.82);
69	        if (block === BlockId.SliverOre && n > 0.86) c = tone([0.93, 0.93, 0.95], 1);
70	        if (block === BlockId.RubyOre && n > 0.88) c = tone([0.86, 0.24, 0.24], 1);
71	        if (block === BlockId.GoldOre && n > 0.84) c = tone([0.96, 0.8, 0.25], 1);
72	        if (block === BlockId.SapphireOre && n > 0.86) c = tone([0.2, 0.62, 0.9], 1);
73	        if (block === BlockId.DiamondOre && n > 0.9) c = tone([0.7, 0.94, 0.98], 1);
74	        if (block === BlockId.Water) c = tone([0.22, 0.48, 0.85], 0.95 + n * 0.12, face === "top" ? 0.02 : 0);
75	        if (block === BlockId.Sand && n > 0.84) c = tone(base, 1.12);
76	        if (block === BlockId.Cactus && face === "side" && x % 4 === 0) c = tone(base, 0.72);
77	        if (block === BlockId.Snow) c = tone(base, 0.97 + n * 0.06);
78	        if (block === BlockId.Bed && face === "top" && y < 5) c = tone([0.95, 0.95, 0.97], 0.95 + n * 0.1); // pillow band
79	        if (block >= BlockId.WheatStage0 && block <= BlockId.WheatStage3 && face === "side" && x % 3 === 1) c = tone(base, 0.66); // gaps read as stalks
80	        if (block === BlockId.Furnace && face === "side" && x >= 5 && x <= 10 && y >= 8 && y <= 12) c = tone([0.95, 0.45, 0.12], 0.85 + n * 0.3); // glowing mouth
81	        if (block === BlockId.Chest) {
82	          if ((x + y) % 4 === 0) c = tone(base, 0.82); // plank grain
83	          if (face === "top" && y === 8) c = tone(base, 0.6); // lid seam
84	          if (face === "side" && y === 7) c = tone(base, 0.58); // lid joint band
85	          if (face === "side" && x >= 7 && x <= 8 && y >= 6 && y <= 9) c = tone([0.62, 0.64, 0.68], 0.9 + n * 0.2); // metal latch
86	        }
87	        if (block === BlockId.MossyCobblestone) {
88	          if (n > 0.8) c = tone([0.5, 0.52, 0.5], 1.05); // pale cobble chunks
89	          if (n < 0.32) c = tone([0.2, 0.4, 0.16], 0.85 + n * 0.5); // moss patches in the cracks
90	        }
91	        if (block === BlockId.Spawner) {
92	          // A dark iron cage over a faint ember core — reads as a Minecraft spawner.
93	          const bar = x % 5 === 0 || y % 5 === 0;
94	          c = bar ? tone([0.32, 0.34, 0.4], 0.85 + n * 0.25) : tone([0.5, 0.16, 0.12], 0.5 + n * 0.6);
95	        }
96	        if (block === BlockId.Lava) {
97	          // Molten rock: a hot orange bed with brighter cracks and dark crust
98	          // flecks. The block emits max light, so it reads as glowing.
99	          const crack = n > 0.78;
100	          const crust = n < 0.2;
101	          c = crack ? tone([1, 0.78, 0.2], 1) : crust ? tone([0.35, 0.1, 0.04], 1) : tone([0.92, 0.34, 0.08], 0.85 + n * 0.4);
102	        }
103	        if (block === BlockId.Torch) {
104	          // A wooden stick on a dark ground with a bright flame near the top —
105	          // the block self-illuminates, so the flame reads as the light source.
106	          const onStick = x >= 7 && x <= 8 && y >= 6;
107	          const flame = x >= 6 && x <= 9 && y <= 6 && Math.abs(x - 7.5) + y * 0.5 < 4;
108	          if (face === "top")
109	            c = tone([1, 0.86, 0.32], 0.8 + n * 0.5); // looking down at the flame
110	          else if (flame) c = tone([1, 0.83, 0.3], 0.78 + n * 0.55);
111	          else if (onStick) c = tone([0.55, 0.38, 0.2], 0.85 + n * 0.2);
112	          else c = tone([0.05, 0.05, 0.07], 1);
113	        }
114	        if (isDoorBlock(block)) {
115	          const state = doorState(block)!;
116	          const panelY = state.upper ? y : y + ATLAS_TILE_SIZE;
117	          const border = x < 2 || x > 13 || panelY < 2 || panelY > 29;
118	          const inset = x >= 4 && x <= 11 && ((panelY >= 4 && panelY <= 13) || (panelY >= 18 && panelY <= 27));
119	          if (border) c = tone(base, 0.62);
120	          else if (inset) c = tone(base, 0.78 + n * 0.08);
121	          else c = tone(base, 0.95 + n * 0.12);
122	          if (!state.upper && x >= 11 && x <= 12 && y >= 5 && y <= 6) c = tone([0.82, 0.72, 0.36], 0.9 + n * 0.15);
123	        }
124	
125	        ctx.fillStyle = rgb(c);
126	        ctx.fillRect(ox + x, oy + y, 1, 1);
127	      }
128	    }
129	  };
130

133	    drawTile(block, "side");
134	    drawTile(block, "bottom");

1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { BLOCK_COLORS, BlockId } from "./blocks";
3	import { doorState, isDoorBlock } from "./doors";
4	
5	// Procedural texture atlas: one 16x16 tile per block face variant, painted on a
6	// canvas at startup. This is the only world module that touches the DOM.
7	export const ATLAS_TILE_SIZE = 16;
8	export const ATLAS_FACE_VARIANTS = 3; // top, side, bottom
9	export const ATLAS_COLUMNS = 16;
10	
11	// Tile range derived from the palette so new blocks get tiles automatically —
12	// a hardcoded last-block bound here once left new blocks sampling garbage UVs.
13	export const ATLAS_BLOCK_COUNT = Math.max(...Object.keys(BLOCK_COLORS).map(Number)) + 1;
14	export const ATLAS_ROWS = Math.ceil((ATLAS_BLOCK_COUNT * ATLAS_FACE_VARIANTS) / ATLAS_COLUMNS);
15	
16	let atlasTextureCache: THREE.CanvasTexture | null = null;
17	
18	function clamp01(v: number): number {
19	  return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
20	}
21	
22	function tone(c: [number, number, number], mul: number, add = 0): [number, number, number] {
23	  return [clamp01(c[0] * mul + add), clamp01(c[1] * mul + add), clamp01(c[2] * mul + add)];
24	}
25	
26	function rgb(c: [number, number, number]): string {
27	  return `rgb(${Math.floor(clamp01(c[0]) * 255)}, ${Math.floor(clamp01(c[1]) * 255)}, ${Math.floor(clamp01(c[2]) * 255)})`;
28	}
29	
30	export function tileIndexFor(block: number, face: "top" | "side" | "bottom"): number {
31	  const faceId = face === "top" ? 0 : face === "side" ? 1 : 2;
32	  return block * ATLAS_FACE_VARIANTS + faceId;
33	}
34	
35	export function createBlockAtlasTexture(): THREE.CanvasTexture {
36	  if (atlasTextureCache) return atlasTextureCache;
37	
38	  const width = ATLAS_COLUMNS * ATLAS_TILE_SIZE;
39	  const height = ATLAS_ROWS * ATLAS_TILE_SIZE;
40	  const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");

282	function placeOres(world: VoxelWorld, rand: () => number): void {
283	  const hasNearbyAir = (x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean => {
284	    return (
285	      world.get(x + 1, y, z) === BlockId.Air ||
286	      world.get(x - 1, y, z) === BlockId.Air ||
287	      world.get(x, y + 1, z) === BlockId.Air ||
288	      world.get(x, y - 1, z) === BlockId.Air ||
289	      world.get(x, y, z + 1) === BlockId.Air ||
290	      world.get(x, y, z - 1) === BlockId.Air ||
291	      world.get(x + 1, y, z) === BlockId.Water ||
292	      world.get(x - 1, y, z) === BlockId.Water ||
293	      world.get(x, y + 1, z) === BlockId.Water ||
294	      world.get(x, y - 1, z) === BlockId.Water ||
295	      world.get(x, y, z + 1) === BlockId.Water ||
296	      world.get(x, y, z - 1) === BlockId.Water
297	    );
298	  };
299	
300	  const placeOreVein = (x: number, y: number, z: number, ore: BlockId, minSize: number, maxSize: number) => {
301	    const size = minSize + Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(1, maxSize - minSize + 1));
302	    for (let i = 0; i < size; i += 1) {
303	      const vx = x + Math.floor((rand() - 0.5) * 4);
304	      const vy = y + Math.floor((rand() - 0.5) * 3);
305	      const vz = z + Math.floor((rand() - 0.5) * 4);
306	      if (!world.inBounds(vx, vy, vz) || vy <= 1) continue;
307	      const b = world.get(vx, vy, vz);
308	      if (b === BlockId.Stone || b === BlockId.Cobblestone) world.set(vx, vy, vz, ore);
309	    }
310	  };
311	
312	  for (const config of GEN.oreConfigs) {
313	    for (let i = 0; i < config.attempts; i += 1) {
314	      const x = 8 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeX - 16));
315	      const y = config.minY + Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(2, world.sizeY - config.maxYOffset));
316	      const z = 8 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeZ - 16));
317	      const block = world.get(x, y, z);
318	      if ((block !== BlockId.Stone && block !== BlockId.Cobblestone) || !hasNearbyAir(x, y, z)) continue;
319	      placeOreVein(x, y, z, config.id, config.minSize, config.maxSize);
320	    }
321	  }

166	// The deterministic world-generation baseline. Bumped whenever worldgen
167	// changes, so old block-diffs (which index against generated terrain) can't be
168	// applied to a different baseline: v6 added dungeons; v7 added deep-cave lava
169	// lakes. Each world records the WORLDGEN_VERSION it was generated under; a world
170	// whose recorded version differs from this constant has its block-diffs
171	// discarded and is rebooted from its stored seed (lib/game/worlds.ts). The save
172	// *schema* (SaveData) is independent and stays at version 5 — lighting is a
173	// derived cache and lava is worldgen, so neither is persisted.

The save *schema* (SaveData) is independent and stays at\n// version 5 — lighting is a derived cache and lava is worldgen, so neither is\n// persisted.\nexport const WORLDGEN_VERSION = 8;"

47	function groundHeight(world: VoxelWorld, x: number, z: number): number {
48	  for (let y = world.sizeY - 1; y >= 0; y -= 1) {

123	export function generateWorld(world: VoxelWorld, worldType: WorldType = "default"): void {
124	  // Fork on the type up front, before any block write: "default" yields the GEN
125	  // values so its output is byte-identical to before this feature.
126	  const cfg = terrainConfigFor(worldType);
127	
128	  const rand = (() => {
129	    let t = (world.seed >>> 0) + 0x6d2b79f5;
130	    return () => {
131	      t += 0x6d2b79f5;
132	      let r = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), 1 | t);
133	      r ^= r + Math.imul(r ^ (r >>> 7), 61 | r);
134	      return ((r ^ (r >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
135	    };
136	  })();
137	
138	  generateTerrain(world, cfg);
139	  carveCaves(world, rand);
140	  placeWater(world, cfg);
141	  placeBeaches(world, cfg);
142	  placeOres(world, rand);
143	  // After placeOres so it can't shift ore RNG (placeOres gates rand on air
144	  // adjacency, which lava would change), and before placeDungeons so dungeon
145	  // rooms — whose floors can sit at or below lavaLevel — overwrite any lava
146	  // rather than flooding. Trees/cacti/houses only read the surface, far above.
147	  placeLava(world);
148	  placeTrees(world, rand);
149	  placeCacti(world);
150	  placeStructures(world, rand);
151	  placeDungeons(world, cfg);
152	}

> AGENT

The order reveals the issue: `placeOres` shares `rand` with `placeTrees`/`placeStructures` that follow it, so adding coal to the shared ore loop shifts the surface (trees moved). The lava precedent kept the surface byte-identical precisely because `placeLava` takes no `rand`. I'll do the same — give coal its own independent PRNG pass so existing ores, trees, and structures stay byte-identical and only coal is added underground. Let me revert the oreConfigs change:

285	}
286	
287	function placeOres(world: VoxelWorld, rand: () => number): void {
288	  const hasNearbyAir = (x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean => {
289	    return (
290	      world.get(x + 1, y, z) === BlockId.Air ||
291	      world.get(x - 1, y, z) === BlockId.Air ||
292	      world.get(x, y + 1, z) === BlockId.Air ||
293	      world.get(x, y - 1, z) === BlockId.Air ||
294	      world.get(x, y, z + 1) === BlockId.Air ||
295	      world.get(x, y, z - 1) === BlockId.Air ||
296	      world.get(x + 1, y, z) === BlockId.Water ||
297	      world.get(x - 1, y, z) === BlockId.Water ||
298	      world.get(x, y + 1, z) === BlockId.Water ||
299	      world.get(x, y - 1, z) === BlockId.Water ||
300	      world.get(x, y, z + 1) === BlockId.Water ||
301	      world.get(x, y, z - 1) === BlockId.Water
302	    );
303	  };
304	
305	  const placeOreVein = (x: number, y: number, z: number, ore: BlockId, minSize: number, maxSize: number) => {
306	    const size = minSize + Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(1, maxSize - minSize + 1));
307	    for (let i = 0; i < size; i += 1) {
308	      const vx = x + Math.floor((rand() - 0.5) * 4);
309	      const vy = y + Math.floor((rand() - 0.5) * 3);
310	      const vz = z + Math.floor((rand() - 0.5) * 4);
311	      if (!world.inBounds(vx, vy, vz) || vy <= 1) continue;
312	      const b = world.get(vx, vy, vz);
313	      if (b === BlockId.Stone || b === BlockId.Cobblestone) world.set(vx, vy, vz, ore);
314	    }
315	  };
316	
317	  for (const config of GEN.oreConfigs) {
318	    for (let i = 0; i < config.attempts; i += 1) {
319	      const x = 8 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeX - 16));
320	      const y = config.minY + Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(2, world.sizeY - config.maxYOffset));
321	      const z = 8 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeZ - 16));
322	      const block = world.get(x, y, z);
323	      if ((block !== BlockId.Stone && block !== BlockId.Cobblestone) || !hasNearbyAir(x, y, z)) continue;
324	      placeOreVein(x, y, z, config.id, config.minSize, config.maxSize);
325	    }
326	  }

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/world/generation.ts",
  "old_string": "  for (const config of GEN.oreConfigs) {\n    for (let i = 0; i < config.attempts; i += 1) {\n      const x = 8 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeX - 16));\n      const y = config.minY + Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(2, world.sizeY - config.maxYOffset));\n      const z = 8 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeZ - 16));\n      const block = world.get(x, y, z);\n      if ((block !== BlockId.Stone && block !== BlockId.Cobblestone) || !hasNearbyAir(x, y, z)) continue;\n      placeOreVein(x, y, z, config.id, config.minSize, config.maxSize);\n    }\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "  for (const config of GEN.oreConfigs) {\n    for (let i = 0; i < config.attempts; i += 1) {\n      const x = 8 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeX - 16));\n      const y = config.minY + Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(2, world.sizeY - config.maxYOffset));\n      const z = 8 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeZ - 16));\n      const block = world.get(x, y, z);\n      if ((block !== BlockId.Stone && block !== BlockId.Cobblestone) || !hasNearbyAir(x, y, z)) continue;\n      placeOreVein(x, y, z, config.id, config.minSize, config.maxSize);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n/** A coal-only PRNG seeded from the world seed, decoupled from the main gen stream. */\nfunction coalRand(seed: number): () => number {\n  let t = (seed ^ 0x85ebca6b) >>> 0;\n  return () => {\n    t += 0x6d2b79f5;\n    let r = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), 1 | t);\n    r ^= r + Math.imul(r ^ (r >>> 7), 61 | r);\n    return ((r ^ (r >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Coal veins, placed on their own PRNG so the pass never consumes from the shared\n * `rand` stream — every other ore, tree, and structure stays byte-identical to a\n * coal-free world. Like the other ores, coal only replaces stone/cobblestone.\n */\nfunction placeCoal(world: VoxelWorld): void {\n  const rand = coalRand(world.seed);\n  const cfg = GEN.coalConfig;\n  const placeVein = (x: number, y: number, z: number) => {\n    const size = cfg.minSize + Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(1, cfg.maxSize - cfg.minSize + 1));\n    for (let i = 0; i < size; i += 1) {\n      const vx = x + Math.floor((rand() - 0.5) * 4);\n      const vy = y + Math.floor((rand() - 0.5) * 3);\n      const vz = z + Math.floor((rand() - 0.5) * 4);\n      if (!world.inBounds(vx, vy, vz) || vy <= 1) continue;\n      const b = world.get(vx, vy, vz);\n      if (b === BlockId.Stone || b === BlockId.Cobblestone) world.set(vx, vy, vz, BlockId.CoalOre);\n    }\n  };\n  for (let i = 0; i < cfg.attempts; i += 1) {\n    const x = 8 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeX - 16));\n    const y = cfg.minY + Math.floor(rand() * Math.max(2, world.sizeY - cfg.maxYOffset));\n    const z = 8 + Math.floor(rand() * (world.sizeZ - 16));\n    const block = world.get(x, y, z);\n    if (block !== BlockId.Stone && block !== BlockId.Cobblestone) continue;\n    placeVein(x, y, z);\n  }\n}"
}

233	        for (let z = 0; z < world.sizeZ; z += 4) {

256	          const nearCenter = Math.hypot(x - world.sizeX / 2, z - world.sizeZ / 2) <= 90;

258	            const block = world.get(x, y, z);
