110	`SPEAR_STUCK_SECONDS`, `SPEAR_THROW_COOLDOWN_SECONDS`, `SPEAR_HIT_RADIUS`.
111	
112	Read by `systems/mining.ts`, `systems/combat.ts`, and `systems/spears.ts`.
113	`MINING_RATE` × a tool's
114	`minePower` (from `items.ts`) ÷ block hardness = break time, so this constant scales
115	_all_ mining globally while item tiers scale it per-tool. Note the deliberate
116	asymmetry: `MINE_REACH` (7) is longer than `ATTACK_REACH` (4.5) — you can dig
117	farther than you can punch. `ATTACK_AIM_DOT` (0.89) is how precisely the crosshair
118	must point at a mob to hit it — lower is more forgiving. The per-ore **tool-tier
119	gate** itself lives in `systems/mining.ts` (`canMineBlock`), not config. Spears
120	override only melee reach; their projectile speed, gravity, lifetime, cooldown,
121	terrain embed duration, and collision radius are global, while tier
122	damage/durability live in `items.ts`.

196	  creation. When a deliberate terrain change invalidates old block-diffs, bump this:
197	  every world whose recorded version differs discards its stale diffs and reboots from
198	  its seed — per-world, without renaming any key (see [save-format.md](save-format.md)).
199	  This replaced the old whole-store `SAVE_KEY` bump, which reset _every_ world at once.
200	  It's versioned independently of the save **schema** (currently v5); don't bump it to
201	  express a schema change — add a migration instead.
202	- **`SAVE_KEY`** (`"minecraft_save_v7"`) is now **legacy**: each world has its own
203	  `minecraft_world_save_<id>` key, and `SAVE_KEY` is read only once by the one-time
204	  migration. Leave it alone.
205	- **`INVENTORY_SLOTS` / `HOTBAR_SLOTS` / `MAX_STACK_SIZE`** affect the saved
206	  inventory layout. `save.ts` already migrates the v1 (40-slot) → v2 (36-slot)
207	  change; altering these again needs a matching migration or old saves break.
208	- **Daylight thresholds and `WAKE_DAY_PHASE`** shape the persisted `dayClock`'s
209	  meaning but are not save-format-breaking on their own.
210	- **Worldgen is not tuned here.** Terrain/ore/structure constants live in the
211	  frozen `GEN` object in `generation.ts` and are a byte-identical save contract
212	  pinned by hash tests — changing them requires the re-baseline policy in
213	  [testing.md](testing.md). This includes **`GEN.dungeonCount`** (28, how many
214	  dungeon rooms are attempted) and the dungeon loot tables / tier odds in
215	  `lib/game/dungeonLoot.ts` (loot is pure logic, not a worldgen byte contract, but
216	  changing the _placement_ count or geometry is).
217	- **World types** (Default / Superflat / Amplified / Islands) are terrain-config
218	  variations of `GEN` in **`terrainConfigFor`** (`generation.ts`) — they change
219	  only sea level and per-biome surface height (base + noise amplitude). `"default"`
220	  returns the GEN values verbatim, so it stays byte-identical; each non-default
221	  type has its own pinned hash. Tuning a type's numbers (e.g. the Islands sea level,
222	  or Amplified's amplitude multiplier) is a deliberate re-baseline for that type
223	  and bumps `WORLDGEN_VERSION` (which discards stale worlds of every type). Islands
224	  values are constrained by spawn: keep land biomes gently sloped so
225	  `findSpawnOnLand` still lands the player on dry ground.
226	
```

5:`SaveData` (version 5) in `lib/game/types.ts`:
19:Each world's `SaveData` is stored in its own localStorage key, `minecraft_world_save_<worldId>` (`worldSaveKey` in `lib/game/worlds.ts`). Read/write and restore validation live in `lib/game/save.ts` — `readSave`/`writeSave` take the key as a parameter and the `Storage` is injectable for tests; `GameEngine.serialize()` produces the save from live state, and `lib/game/engine/blockChanges.ts` maintains the block diff. (The legacy single-world key `SAVE_KEY` = `minecraft_save_v7` is now read only once, by the migration described under [Profiles & worlds](#profiles--worlds).)
25:Continuous-water exposure, lava burn, and the oxygen/drowning meter all live only
31:- **v5** (unchanged for caves & lighting) — the lighting system adds no persisted state (light is a derived cache, recomputed at load), and lava is worldgen, not a saved field. So the save _schema_ stays at version 5. But the deep-cave lava changes the deterministic terrain baseline, so **`SAVE_KEY` is bumped `minecraft_save_v6` → `minecraft_save_v7`**: old block-diffs were computed against the lava-free world and must never be applied to the new one. Pre-lava saves are discarded in practice. Torches are additive items/blocks, persisted by string id / in the block diff like any other placed block.
32:- **v5** (unchanged for ranged combat & the endgame boss) — bows, arrows, the Cursed Totem, the Dragon Heart, and the Dragon Sword are **additive items**, persisted by string id in the existing inventory/container slots, so no schema change was needed. **Arrows in flight, the boss, its minions, and the `victory` flag are all transient** (never serialized — like mobs), and there is no worldgen change (the boss is summoned, not generated), so neither the `version` nor `SAVE_KEY` moved.
46:- **`minecraft_world_save_<worldId>`** — one `SaveData` blob per world (the schema above), read/written through `readSave`/`writeSave`.
48:`worldgenVersion` records the `WORLDGEN_VERSION` (`lib/game/config.ts`, currently 7) the world was generated under. This **replaces the old whole-store `SAVE_KEY` bump**: when the worldgen baseline changes, bump `WORLDGEN_VERSION`, and a world whose recorded version differs has its stale block-diffs discarded and reboots from its stored seed — per-world, with no key rename and without touching unaffected worlds.
50:**World types.** A world's `worldType` selects a generation preset (`lib/world/worldTypes.ts`). `generateWorld(world, worldType)` forks on it before any block write (`terrainConfigFor`, `lib/world/generation.ts`), varying only sea level and per-biome surface height; everything else reads `GEN`. The `"default"` branch returns the GEN values verbatim, so default worlds stay **byte-identical** to the pre-feature generator — the existing determinism hashes are unchanged. Each non-default type is its own deterministic save contract with its own SHA-256 baseline in `generation.test.ts`; `worldgenVersion` still guards all types (a deliberate change to any type's config bumps `WORLDGEN_VERSION`, discarding stale worlds of that vintage).
56:`migrateLegacySave` (`lib/game/legacyMigration.ts`) runs once on first boot, gated on the **absence** of `minecraft_profiles_v1` (its presence is the already-migrated flag, which survives a rollback that re-creates the legacy key). When a legacy `minecraft_save_v7` exists it creates a default "Player" profile (carrying the old global skin) and **copies** the save into one "My World" blob, then removes the legacy key — the copy precedes the delete so a mid-migration failure can never lose the original. A brand-new player with **no** legacy save gets nothing here; the menu opens straight into the create-profile form (`ProfileSelect` renders it whenever there are zero profiles), so their first act is naming and skinning a profile.
64:- Saves store **diffs against generated terrain**, so changing world generation or the voxel index formula (`x + z*sizeX + y*sizeX*sizeZ`) silently corrupts existing saves — bump `WORLDGEN_VERSION` when you do, and each world discards its stale diffs (rebooting from its seed) on the mismatch.
65:- Worldgen output is pinned by SHA-256 characterization tests in `lib/world/generation.test.ts`; they fail on any byte-level change. See [testing.md](testing.md) for the re-baseline policy. Caveat: the noise functions use `Math.sin`, whose exact results are engine-defined — the tests prove refactor purity on the pinned Bun version, not cross-browser save portability.
```

29	### Version history
30	
31	- **v5** (unchanged for caves & lighting) — the lighting system adds no persisted state (light is a derived cache, recomputed at load), and lava is worldgen, not a saved field. So the save _schema_ stays at version 5. But the deep-cave lava changes the deterministic terrain baseline, so **`SAVE_KEY` is bumped `minecraft_save_v6` → `minecraft_save_v7`**: old block-diffs were computed against the lava-free world and must never be applied to the new one. Pre-lava saves are discarded in practice. Torches are additive items/blocks, persisted by string id / in the block diff like any other placed block.
32	- **v5** (unchanged for ranged combat & the endgame boss) — bows, arrows, the Cursed Totem, the Dragon Heart, and the Dragon Sword are **additive items**, persisted by string id in the existing inventory/container slots, so no schema change was needed. **Arrows in flight, the boss, its minions, and the `victory` flag are all transient** (never serialized — like mobs), and there is no worldgen change (the boss is summoned, not generated), so neither the `version` nor `SAVE_KEY` moved.
33	- **v5** — adds optional `lootedChests` (opened/broken dungeon loot chests). `migrateSaveV4toV5` is a pure version bump (absent → no dungeon chests yet looted). Unlike v3/v4, **`SAVE_KEY` is bumped to `minecraft_save_v6`** here — not because of the save _shape_ (the field is additive) but because the dungeon worldgen changed the deterministic terrain baseline, so old block-diffs would index against the wrong world. Pre-dungeon saves are therefore discarded in practice; the migration keeps the version chain complete regardless. `readSave` chains v1 → v2 → v3 → v4 → v5.
34	- **v4** — adds optional `blockEntities` (chest contents). `migrateSaveV3toV4` is a pure version bump (absent → restores as no containers), so **`SAVE_KEY` was unchanged** at the time (player-placed chests only; worldgen and the index formula were untouched) and v1/v2/v3 saves load fine.
```

  "new_string": "Underground is now genuinely **dark**: sunlight reaches down open shafts and\nspills a little way into cave mouths, but the deep tunnels are pitch black. Craft\n**torches** (1 coal + 1 wood → 4 torches) and place them as you explore — each casts\na warm pool of light that won't fade at night. Without a light source you're mining\nblind, and the dark is where monsters lurk. **Coal** is the shallow, common ore you\nmine for torch and furnace fuel — if you're out, smelt a log into **charcoal**, which\nworks the same."
}
```

184	**Tools gate what you can mine.** A pickaxe's tier determines which blocks it can
185	harvest at all — mining with too weak a tool yields nothing:
186	
187	| To mine…                  | You need at least… |
188	| ------------------------- | ------------------ |
189	| Stone, cobblestone, brick | Wood pickaxe       |
190	| Sliver ore                | Stone pickaxe      |
191	| Ruby & gold ore           | Sliver pickaxe     |
192	| Sapphire & diamond ore    | Ruby pickaxe       |
193	
194	Higher-tier pickaxes (sapphire, gold, diamond) also mine **faster**. Dirt, sand,
195	wood, leaves, and crops need no special tool. Ores hide underground and in caves;

id: toolu_011BeWuHxyTY7R1pgMX4xez6
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/CHANGELOG.md",
  "old_string": "### Added\n\n- **Cow & pig farm animals**: two new passive mobs join sheep/chicken/horse, following the same template.",
  "new_string": "### Added\n\n- **Coal & a fuel economy**: a new shallow ore deepens the early game and gives the furnace a real fuel. **Coal ore** is the most common ore (large veins reaching near the surface, mineable with a wood pickaxe) and drops the **coal** fuel item directly. **Charcoal** smelts from a single log as a coal substitute, so a player who hasn't struck coal can still cook. Both fuel the furnace's cooking recipes (chicken/mutton/beef/porkchop) and **torches now cost coal + wood** instead of wood alone. All procedural, **zero new assets** (a dark-flecked atlas tile + a recolored sprite lump)\n  - Coal is placed in its own pass (`placeCoal`) on a **dedicated PRNG**, so it never consumes from the shared worldgen `rand` stream — every other ore, tree, and structure stays **byte-identical** to a coal-free world, exactly as deep-cave lava did. Only underground coal cells differ; the meshing and structural-probe tests stay green and only the byte hashes were re-rolled\n  - **Worldgen re-baseline**: the new ore changes the deterministic terrain baseline, so **`WORLDGEN_VERSION` is bumped `7` → `8`** — a world whose recorded version differs discards its stale block-diffs and reboots from its seed. The save **schema** stays version 5 (coal/charcoal are additive items, coal ore persists in the block diff). `canMineBlock` gates coal behind a wood pickaxe (tier 1, like stone); `GEN.coalConfig` holds its vein tunables\n- **Cow & pig farm animals**: two new passive mobs join sheep/chicken/horse, following the same template."
}
```

The furnace\nhere is a station gate, not a fuel-burning machine, so \"fuel\" is simply a\nrecipe ingredient — which keeps this change additive and low-risk.\n\n- New CoalOre block: the most common ore (large veins reaching near the\n  surface), mineable with a wood pickaxe, dropping the `coal` fuel item\n  directly (not a placeable ore block). New `coal` and `charcoal` items;\n  charcoal smelts from a single log as a coal substitute so a player without\n  coal can still cook.\n- Cooking recipes (chicken/mutton/beef/porkchop) now burn coal or charcoal,\n  and torches cost 1 coal + 1 wood instead of wood alone. Procedural only: a\n  dark-flecked atlas tile + a recolored sprite lump, zero new assets.\n- Coal is placed on its own PRNG (placeCoal) so it never shifts the shared\n  worldgen rand stream: existing ores, trees, and structures stay\n  byte-identical to a coal-free world — only underground coal differs. The\n  meshing and structural-probe tests stay green; only the byte hashes were\n  re-rolled.\n\nWORLDGEN_VERSION bumps 7 -> 8 (a world on the old baseline discards its stale\nblock-diffs and reboots from its seed). The save schema stays version 5 (coal\nore persists in the block diff, coal/charcoal by string id). Adds canMineBlock\ntier tests, a coal worldgen balance probe, and recipe tests; docs and CHANGELOG\nupdated.\nEOF\ngit log --oneline -3",
  "description": "Commit Phase 2"
}

7	/** Session-only camera presentation mode; never persisted. Gameplay stays eye-relative in all modes. */

12	export function nextCameraMode(mode: CameraMode): CameraMode {
13	  return CAMERA_MODE_CYCLE[(CAMERA_MODE_CYCLE.indexOf(mode) + 1) % CAMERA_MODE_CYCLE.length];
14	}

7	  BOSS_MELEE_DAMAGE,
8	  BOSS_MELEE_REACH,
9	  BOSS_MINION_CAP,
10	  BOSS_SPREAD,
11	  BOSS_SUMMON_INTERVAL_SECONDS,
12	  HOSTILE_BURN_ABOVE_DAYLIGHT,
13	  HOSTILE_CAP,
14	  MOB_ARROW_KNOCKBACK,
15	  SKELETON_ARROW_DAMAGE,
16	  SKELETON_ARROW_SPEED,
17	  SKELETON_FIRE_VGAP,
18	  SKELETON_LEAD_FACTOR,
19	  SKELETON_STANDOFF_MAX,
20	  SKELETON_STANDOFF_MIN,
21	  SPIDER_AGGRO_BELOW_DAYLIGHT
22	} from "@/lib/game/config";
23	import { MOB_TEMPLATES } from "@/lib/game/mobs";
24	import type { EmitGameEvent, GameState, MobState } from "../state";
25	import { spawnArrow } from "../projectiles";

29	// Scratch vectors — per-frame tick over every mob must not allocate.
30	const UP = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);
31	const scratchToPlayer = new THREE.Vector3();
32	const scratchToPlayer3D = new THREE.Vector3();
33	const scratchMobEye = new THREE.Vector3();
34	const scratchPlayerAim = new THREE.Vector3();
35	const scratchRay = new THREE.Vector3();
36	const scratchMobFeet = new THREE.Vector3();
37	const scratchAim = new THREE.Vector3();
38	
39	/**
40	 * A ranged mob looses an arrow from its eye toward the player's chest, leading a
41	 * moving target by a fraction of the arrow's travel time. The arrow is not
42	 * player-owned, so it only ever hits the player (never the firer or other mobs).
43	 */
44	function fireMobArrow(state: GameState, mob: MobState, damage: number, speed: number, emit: EmitGameEvent): void {
45	  const { player } = state;
46	  const eyeY = mob.position.y + mob.halfHeight * 0.7;
47	  const dist = Math.hypot(player.position.x - mob.position.x, player.position.z - mob.position.z);
48	  const lead = (dist / speed) * SKELETON_LEAD_FACTOR;
49	  scratchAim.set(
50	    player.position.x + player.velocity.x * lead - mob.position.x,
51	    player.position.y + 0.9 - eyeY,
52	    player.position.z + player.velocity.z * lead - mob.position.z
53	  );
54	  spawnArrow(state, mob.position.x, eyeY, mob.position.z, scratchAim, {
55	    speed,
56	    damage,
57	    knockback: MOB_ARROW_KNOCKBACK,
58	    fromPlayer: false,
59	    ttl: ARROW_TTL
60	  });
61	  emit({ type: "mobAttacked", kind: mob.kind });
62	}
63	
64	/** The boss looses a 3-arrow horizontal spread aimed at the player's chest. */
65	function fireBossSpread(state: GameState, mob: MobState, emit: EmitGameEvent): void {
66	  const { player } = state;
67	  const eyeY = mob.position.y + mob.halfHeight * 0.7;
68	  const baseX = player.position.x - mob.position.x;
69	  const baseY = player.position.y + 0.9 - eyeY;
70	  const baseZ = player.position.z - mob.position.z;
71	  for (let spread = -1; spread <= 1; spread += 1) {
72	    scratchAim.set(baseX, baseY, baseZ);
73	    if (spread !== 0) scratchAim.applyAxisAngle(UP, spread * BOSS_SPREAD);
74	    spawnArrow(state, mob.position.x, eyeY, mob.position.z, scratchAim, {
75	      speed: BOSS_ARROW_SPEED,
76	      damage: BOSS_ARROW_DAMAGE,
77	      knockback: MOB_ARROW_KNOCKBACK,
78	      fromPlayer: false,
79	      ttl: ARROW_TTL
80	    });
81	  }
82	  emit({ type: "mobAttacked", kind: mob.kind });
83	}
84	
85	/**
86	 * The boss periodically conjures a skeleton or zombie nearby while it is engaged,
87	 * up to its own minion cap and the shared global hostile cap.
88	 */
89	function tickBossSummon(state: GameState, mob: MobState, dt: number, deps: MobTickDeps): void {
90	  mob.summonTimer = (mob.summonTimer ?? 0) - dt;
91	  if (mob.summonTimer > 0) return;
92	  mob.summonTimer = BOSS_SUMMON_INTERVAL_SECONDS;
93	
94	  let minions = 0;
95	  let hostiles = 0;
96	  for (const other of state.mobs) {
97	    if (!other.hostile) continue;
98	    hostiles += 1;
99	    if (other.kind !== "boss") minions += 1;
100	  }
101	  if (minions >= BOSS_MINION_CAP || hostiles >= HOSTILE_CAP) return;
102	
103	  const kind = deps.rng() < 0.5 ? "skeleton" : "zombie";
104	  const angle = deps.rng() * Math.PI * 2;
105	  const radius = 2 + deps.rng() * 2;
106	  const mx = mob.position.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius;
107	  const mz = mob.position.z + Math.sin(angle) * radius;
108	  const my = deps.surfaceYAt(mx, mz);
109	  pushMob(state, kind, true, mx, my, mz, deps.rng);
110	  deps.emit({ type: "mobSpawned", kind, x: mx, y: my, z: mz });
111	}
112	
113	export type MobTickDeps = {
114	  surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn;
115	  applyDamage: (amount: number) => void;
116	  removeMobAt: (index: number) => void;
117	  rng: () => number;
118	  emit: EmitGameEvent;
119	};
120	
121	export function tickMobs(state: GameState, dt: number, deps: MobTickDeps): void {
122	  const { world, daylight, mobs, isDead } = state;
123	  const playerPosition = state.player.position;
124	  const playerVelocity = state.player.velocity;
125	  const deadIndices: number[] = [];
126	
127	  for (let i = 0; i < mobs.length; i += 1) {
128	    const mob = mobs[i];
129	    mob.attackTimer -= dt;
130	    mob.turnTimer -= dt;
131	    const activeHostile = mob.hostile && (mob.kind !== "spider" || daylight < SPIDER_AGGRO_BELOW_DAYLIGHT);
132	
133	    scratchToPlayer.copy(playerPosition).sub(mob.position).setY(0);
134	    const distanceToPlayer = scratchToPlayer.length();
135	    scratchToPlayer3D.copy(playerPosition).sub(mob.position);
136	    const attackDistance = scratchToPlayer3D.length();
137	    const verticalGap = Math.abs(scratchToPlayer3D.y);
138	    let moveSpeed = mob.speed;
139	    const isBoss = mob.kind === "boss";
140	    const isRanged = MOB_TEMPLATES[mob.kind].ranged === true;
141	    let moveSign = 1;
142	
143	    if (activeHostile && distanceToPlayer < mob.detectRange) {
144	      if (distanceToPlayer > 0.001) mob.direction.lerp(scratchToPlayer.normalize(), 0.2).normalize();
145	      moveSpeed *= 1.15;
146	      // Skeletons kite (back off / hold in a standoff band); the boss bears down.
147	      if (isRanged && !isBoss) {
148	        if (distanceToPlayer < SKELETON_STANDOFF_MIN) moveSign = -1;
149	        else if (distanceToPlayer <= SKELETON_STANDOFF_MAX) moveSign = 0;
150	      }
151	    } else if (!mob.hostile && distanceToPlayer < 4.2) {
152	      if (distanceToPlayer > 0.001) mob.direction.lerp(scratchToPlayer.normalize().multiplyScalar(-1), 0.2).normalize();
153	      moveSpeed *= 1.15;
154	    } else if (mob.turnTimer <= 0) {
155	      mob.direction.applyAxisAngle(UP, (deps.rng() - 0.5) * Math.PI).normalize();
156	      mob.turnTimer = 1.5 + deps.rng() * 4;
157	    }
158	    mob.moveSpeed = moveSpeed;
159	
160	    let nx = mob.position.x + mob.direction.x * moveSpeed * dt * moveSign;
161	    let nz = mob.position.z + mob.direction.z * moveSpeed * dt * moveSign;
162	
163	    if (nx < 2 || nz < 2 || nx > world.sizeX - 2 || nz > world.sizeZ - 2) {
164	      mob.direction.multiplyScalar(-1);
165	      nx = mob.position.x + mob.direction.x * moveSpeed * dt * moveSign;
166	      nz = mob.position.z + mob.direction.z * moveSpeed * dt * moveSign;
167	      mob.turnTimer = 1;
168	    }
169	
170	    let ground = deps.surfaceYAt(nx, nz);
171	    scratchMobFeet.set(nx, ground, nz);
172	    if (collidesAt(world, scratchMobFeet, 0.28, mob.halfHeight * 2)) {
173	      mob.direction.multiplyScalar(-1);
174	      nx = mob.position.x;
175	      nz = mob.position.z;
176	      ground = mob.position.y - mob.halfHeight;
177	      mob.turnTimer = 1;
178	    }
179	    mob.position.set(nx, ground + mob.halfHeight, nz);
180	    mob.yaw = Math.atan2(mob.direction.x, mob.direction.z);
181	
182	    // Ranged mobs shoot from across their detect range; melee mobs (and the boss
183	    // up close) must be adjacent. The boss reaches a little farther for melee.
184	    const meleeReach = isBoss ? BOSS_MELEE_REACH : 4;
185	    const meleeVGap = isBoss ? SKELETON_FIRE_VGAP : 1.6;
186	    const meleeReady = activeHostile && attackDistance < meleeReach && verticalGap < meleeVGap;
187	    const fireReady = isRanged && activeHostile && attackDistance < mob.detectRange && verticalGap < SKELETON_FIRE_VGAP;
188	
189	    let hasLineOfSight = true;
190	    if (meleeReady || fireReady) {
191	      scratchMobEye.set(mob.position.x, mob.position.y + mob.halfHeight * 0.35, mob.position.z);
192	      scratchPlayerAim.set(playerPosition.x, playerPosition.y + 0.9, playerPosition.z);
193	      scratchRay.copy(scratchPlayerAim).sub(scratchMobEye);
194	      if (scratchRay.lengthSq() > 1e-6) {
195	        const hit = voxelRaycast(world, scratchMobEye, scratchRay.normalize(), attackDistance + 0.5);
196	        hasLineOfSight = hit === null;
197	      }
198	    }
199	
200	    // !isDead: mobs keep ticking through the respawn countdown, but attacking a
201	    // corpse should neither sound nor re-arm the attack cooldown.
202	    if (!isDead && (meleeReady || fireReady) && hasLineOfSight && mob.attackTimer <= 0) {
203	      // The boss bites when adjacent, otherwise looses a spread; skeletons only
204	      // ever shoot; melee mobs only ever bite.
205	      const doMelee = meleeReady && (!isRanged || isBoss);
206	      if (doMelee) {
207	        deps.emit({ type: "mobAttacked", kind: mob.kind });
208	        deps.applyDamage(isBoss ? BOSS_MELEE_DAMAGE : mob.attackDamage);
209	        if (distanceToPlayer > 0.001) {
210	          scratchToPlayer.normalize().multiplyScalar(isBoss ? 6 : 4.2);
211	          playerVelocity.x += scratchToPlayer.x;
212	          playerVelocity.z += scratchToPlayer.z;
213	          playerVelocity.y = Math.max(playerVelocity.y, isBoss ? 4.5 : 3.4);
214	        }
215	      } else if (fireReady) {
216	        if (isBoss) fireBossSpread(state, mob, deps.emit);
217	        else fireMobArrow(state, mob, SKELETON_ARROW_DAMAGE, SKELETON_ARROW_SPEED, deps.emit);
218	      }
219	      mob.attackTimer = mob.attackCooldown;
220	    }
221	
222	    // The boss conjures minions while it is engaged with the player.
223	    if (!isDead && isBoss && activeHostile && distanceToPlayer < mob.detectRange) {
224	      tickBossSummon(state, mob, dt, deps);
225	    }
226	
227	    // Zombies and skeletons burn in broad daylight; the boss is immune.
228	    if (mob.hostile && mob.kind !== "spider" && mob.kind !== "boss" && daylight > HOSTILE_BURN_ABOVE_DAYLIGHT) {
229	      mob.hp -= dt * 0.8;
230	    }
231	
232	    if (mob.hp <= 0) deadIndices.push(i);
233	  }
234	
235	  for (let i = deadIndices.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) deps.removeMobAt(deadIndices[i]);
236	}
237

1	import { applyEdit, BlockId, VoxelWorld } from "@/lib/world";
2	
3	/**
4	 * Tracks player edits to the generated world as deltas against the worldgen
5	 * baseline — the heart of the save format. A block restored to its original
6	 * value drops out of the delta set, keeping saves minimal.
7	 */
8	export type BlockChangeTracker = {
9	  /** Tracked write: updates the world and the delta set. */
10	  set(x: number, y: number, z: number, block: BlockId): void;
11	  /** Replays saved deltas onto a freshly generated world (boot only). */
12	  applySavedChanges(changes: Array<[number, number]>): void;
13	  /** Current deltas as [voxelIndex, blockId] pairs, ready to persist. */
14	  changes(): Array<[number, number]>;
15	};
16	
17	export function createBlockChangeTracker(world: VoxelWorld): BlockChangeTracker {
18	  const changedBlocks = new Map<number, number>();
19	  const baselineByIndex = new Map<number, number>();
20	
21	  return {
22	    set(x, y, z, block) {
23	      if (!world.inBounds(x, y, z)) return;
24	      const idx = world.index(x, y, z);
25	      if (!baselineByIndex.has(idx)) baselineByIndex.set(idx, world.get(x, y, z));
26	      world.set(x, y, z, block);
27	      // Patch lighting locally now the block changed. This is the single
28	      // chokepoint for every gameplay edit (mining, doors, farming, crop
29	      // growth), so each one relights without the renderer doing anything; the
30	      // load path (applySavedChanges) bypasses this and the full bake covers it.
31	      applyEdit(world, world.light, x, y, z);
32	      const baseline = baselineByIndex.get(idx) ?? BlockId.Air;
33	      if (block === baseline) changedBlocks.delete(idx);
34	      else changedBlocks.set(idx, block);
35	    },
36	
37	    applySavedChanges(changes) {
38	      const layer = world.sizeX * world.sizeZ;
39	      for (const [idx, block] of changes) {
40	        const y = Math.floor(idx / layer);
41	        const rem = idx - y * layer;
42	        const z = Math.floor(rem / world.sizeX);
43	        const x = rem - z * world.sizeX;
44	        if (!world.inBounds(x, y, z)) continue;
45	        world.set(x, y, z, block as BlockId);
46	        changedBlocks.set(idx, block);
47	      }
48	    },
49	
50	    changes() {
51	      return [...changedBlocks.entries()];
52	    }
53	  };
54	}
55	
```

44	function addBlockDrop(state: GameState, block: BlockId, rng: () => number): void {
45	  for (const drop of rollBlockDrops(block, rng)) {
46	    state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, drop.itemId, drop.count) ?? state.inventory;
47	  }
48	}
49	
50	/**
51	 * Empties a broken chest into the inventory. Returns true if the break may
52	 * proceed (the chest was empty, or its contents all fit), false to refuse it
53	 * (no room — the chest is left intact). On success the container entry is
54	 * removed and a pickedUp toast announces what was retrieved.
55	 */
56	function spillChestOnBreak(state: GameState, idx: number, emit: EmitGameEvent): boolean {
57	  // Breaking an unopened dungeon chest still pays out its loot.
58	  fillDungeonChestIfUnlooted(state, idx);
59	  const container = state.containers.get(idx);
60	  const items = container?.filter((slot) => slot.id && slot.count > 0) ?? [];
61	  if (items.length > 0) {
62	    const merged = tryInsertSlots(state.inventory, items);
63	    if (!merged) {
64	      emit({ type: "breakBlocked", reason: "containerFull" });
65	      return false;
66	    }
67	    state.inventory = merged;
68	    const picked = new Map<string, number>();
69	    for (const slot of items) picked.set(slot.id!, (picked.get(slot.id!) ?? 0) + slot.count);
70	    emit({ type: "pickedUp", items: [...picked].map(([itemId, count]) => ({ itemId, count })) });
71	  }
72	  state.containers.delete(idx);
73	  return true;
74	}
75	
76	/** Advances mining progress while the mouse is held; breaks the block at full progress. */
77	export function tickMining(state: GameState, input: FrameInput, dt: number, emit: EmitGameEvent, rng: () => number): void {
78	  if (!input.leftMouseHeld) {
79	    // Releasing the button abandons progress (matching the crack overlay).
80	    if (state.mining.progress > 0) resetMining(state);
81	    return;
82	  }
83	  if (state.inventoryOpen || state.isDead || !input.pointerLocked) return;
84	
85	  const { world, mining } = state;
86	  const origin = eyePosition(state, scratchEye);
87	  const direction = lookDirection(state.player.yaw, state.player.pitch, scratchDir);
88	  const result = voxelRaycast(world, origin, direction, MINE_REACH);
89	  if (!result) {
90	    resetMining(state);
91	    return;
92	  }
93	
94	  const bx = result.hit.x;
95	  const by = result.hit.y;
96	  const bz = result.hit.z;
97	  const targetBlock = world.get(bx, by, bz);
98	  const tool = selectedTool(state);
99	  const tier = tool?.mineTier ?? 0;
100	
101	  if (targetBlock === BlockId.Bedrock || targetBlock === BlockId.Air || !canMineBlock(targetBlock as BlockId, tier)) {
102	    resetMining(state);
103	    return;
104	  }
105	
106	  const key = `${bx},${by},${bz}`;
107	  if (mining.targetKey !== key) {
108	    mining.targetKey = key;
109	    mining.progress = 0;
110	  }
111	
112	  const hardness = BREAK_HARDNESS[targetBlock as BlockId] ?? 2;
113	  mining.progress += dt * miningSpeed(tool) * MINING_RATE;
114	  if (mining.progress < hardness) return;
115	
116	  // Breaking a chest empties it into the inventory first; if it does not all
117	  // fit, refuse the break so nothing is lost (the chest stays intact).
118	  if (targetBlock === BlockId.Chest && !spillChestOnBreak(state, world.index(bx, by, bz), emit)) {
119	    resetMining(state);
120	    return;
121	  }
122	
123	  if (isDoorBlock(targetBlock)) {
124	    const door = doorState(targetBlock)!;
125	    const other = doorState(world.get(bx, door.upper ? by - 1 : by + 1, bz));
126	    state.blockChanges.set(bx, by, bz, BlockId.Air);
127	    if (other && other.upper !== door.upper) {
128	      state.blockChanges.set(bx, door.upper ? by - 1 : by + 1, bz, BlockId.Air);
129	    }
130	  } else {
131	    state.blockChanges.set(bx, by, bz, BlockId.Air);
132	  }
133	  if (tool) state.inventory = consumeToolDurability(state.inventory, state.selectedSlot, 1) ?? state.inventory;
134	  addBlockDrop(state, targetBlock as BlockId, rng);
135	  state.worldMeshDirty = true;
136	  resetMining(state);
137	  emit({ type: "blockBroken", blockId: targetBlock as BlockId, x: bx, y: by, z: bz });
138	}

207	  step(dt: number, input: FrameInput): void {
208	    const state = this.state;
209	    if (state.paused) {
210	      // Full freeze: mobs, the day clock, mining, and stats all stop.
211	      this.refreshSnapshot();
212	      return;
213	    }
214	    state.capsActive = input.capsActive;
215	
216	    // Stuck detection / auto-unstuck.
217	    const inBadState = collidesAt(state.world, state.player.position, PLAYER_HALF_WIDTH, PLAYER_HEIGHT) || state.player.position.y < 2;
218	    state.timers.stuckTimer = inBadState ? state.timers.stuckTimer + dt : 0;
219	    if (state.timers.stuckTimer > STUCK_RESET_SECONDS) {
220	      this.forceUnstuck();
221	      state.timers.stuckTimer = 0;
222	    }
223	
224	    // Death: only mobs and the respawn countdown tick while dead.
225	    if (state.isDead) {
226	      if (tickRespawnTimer(state, dt)) this.respawn();
227	      else {
228	        tickMobs(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
229	        // Keep ticking so in-flight arrows clear; applyDamage no-ops while dead.
230	        tickProjectiles(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
231	      }
232	      this.refreshSnapshot();
233	      return;
234	    }
235	
236	    // Sleeping: a full freeze during the fade, then a jump to the next morning.
237	    if (state.sleepTimer > 0) {
238	      state.sleepTimer = Math.max(0, state.sleepTimer - dt);
239	      if (state.sleepTimer === 0) this.wakeToMorning();
240	      this.refreshSnapshot();
241	      return;
242	    }
243	
244	    const move = tickPlayerMotion(state, input, dt, this.applyDamage);
245	    if (move.didJump) this.emit({ type: "jumped" });
246	    if (move.didLand) this.emit({ type: "landed", impact: move.landImpact });
247	    tickHungerDrain(state, move);
248	    tickHealthRegen(state, dt);
249	    tickWaterExposure(state, dt, this.applyEnvironmentalDamage);
250	    tickLavaExposure(state, dt, this.applyEnvironmentalDamage);
251	    tickOxygen(state, dt, this.applyEnvironmentalDamage);
252	    state.timers.bowCooldownTimer = Math.max(0, state.timers.bowCooldownTimer - dt);
253	    tickMining(state, input, dt, this.emit, this.rng);
254	    tickThrownSpears(state, dt, this.removeMobAt, this.emit);
255	    tickDayNight(state, dt);
256	    tickWeather(state);
257	    tickRandomBlocks(state, dt, this.rng);
258	    tickHostileSpawnDirector(state, dt, this.rng, this.surfaceYAt);
259	    tickSpawnerDirector(state, dt, this.rng, this.emit);
260	    tickMobs(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
261	    tickProjectiles(state, dt, this.mobTickDeps);
262	    tickBreeding(state, dt, this.rng, this.surfaceYAt, this.emit);
263	    this.tickDebugInfo(dt);
264	
265	    this.refreshSnapshot();
266	  }
267

273	        if (command.index >= 0 && command.index < Math.min(HOTBAR_SLOTS, state.inventory.length)) {
274	          state.selectedSlot = command.index;
275	        }
276	        break;

501	  private removeMobAt = (index: number): void => {
502	    const state = this.state;
503	    const mob = state.mobs[index];
504	    state.mobs.splice(index, 1);
505	    // Babies drop nothing — only grown animals yield loot.
506	    const drops = mob.ageTimer <= 0 ? rollMobDrops(mob.kind, this.rng) : [];
507	    for (const drop of drops) {
508	      state.inventory = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, drop.itemId, drop.count) ?? state.inventory;
509	    }
510	    this.emit({ type: "mobDied", kind: mob.kind, x: mob.position.x, y: mob.position.y, z: mob.position.z });
511	    // Drops land straight in inventory (no ground item), so announce them.
512	    if (drops.length > 0) this.emit({ type: "pickedUp", items: drops });
513	    // Defeating the boss is the win condition — fire the one-shot victory.
514	    if (mob.kind === "boss") {
515	      state.victory = true;

535	    state.inventory = inv.adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, "boss_summoner", -1, state.selectedSlot) ?? state.inventory;
536	    this.emit({ type: "bossSummoned", x: point.x, y: point.y, z: point.z });
537	    return true;
538	  }

56	export function tryInteractBlock(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent): boolean {
57	  const { world, player } = state;
58	  scratchEye.set(player.position.x, player.position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, player.position.z);
59	  lookDirection(player.yaw, player.pitch, scratchDir);
60	  const result = voxelRaycast(world, scratchEye, scratchDir, MINE_REACH);
61	  if (!result) return false;
62	
63	  const block = world.get(result.hit.x, result.hit.y, result.hit.z) as BlockId;
64	  const kind = INTERACTIVE_BLOCKS[block];
65	  if (!kind) return false;
66	
67	  if (kind === "bed") return interactBed(state, emit, result.hit.x, result.hit.y, result.hit.z);
68	  if (kind === "furnace") return interactFurnace(state, emit);
69	  if (kind === "chest") return interactChest(state, emit, result.hit.x, result.hit.y, result.hit.z);
70	  if (kind === "door") return interactDoor(state, emit, result.hit.x, result.hit.y, result.hit.z);
71	  return false;
72	}
73	
74	function interactDoor(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent, x: number, y: number, z: number): boolean {
75	  const current = doorState(state.world.get(x, y, z));
76	  if (!current) return false;
77	  const lowerY = current.upper ? y - 1 : y;
78	  const lower = doorState(state.world.get(x, lowerY, z));
79	  const upper = doorState(state.world.get(x, lowerY + 1, z));
80	  if (!lower || lower.upper || !upper?.upper || lower.facing !== upper.facing || lower.open !== upper.open) return true;
81	  state.blockChanges.set(x, lowerY, z, doorBlock(lower.facing, !lower.open, false));
82	  state.blockChanges.set(x, lowerY + 1, z, doorBlock(lower.facing, !lower.open, true));
83	  state.worldMeshDirty = true;
84	  emit({ type: "doorToggled", open: !lower.open });
85	  return true;
86	}

160	
161	/**
162	 * Right-click "use" of the held item on the aimed block: a hoe tills grass/dirt
163	 * into farmland, seeds plant wheat on farmland. Returns true when an action
164	 * happened (consumes the click), false to fall through to block placement.
165	 */
166	export function tryUseHeldItem(state: GameState, emit: EmitGameEvent, rng: () => number): boolean {
167	  void rng; // reserved for future randomized uses (bone meal, …)
168	  const slot = state.inventory[state.selectedSlot];
169	  if (!slot?.id || slot.count <= 0) return false;
170	  const isHoe = slot.id.endsWith("_hoe");
171	  const isSeeds = slot.id === "seeds";
172	  if (!isHoe && !isSeeds) return false;
173	
174	  const { world, player } = state;
175	  scratchEye.set(player.position.x, player.position.y + EYE_HEIGHT, player.position.z);
176	  lookDirection(player.yaw, player.pitch, scratchDir);
177	  const result = voxelRaycast(world, scratchEye, scratchDir, MINE_REACH);
178	  if (!result) return false;
179	  const { x, y, z } = result.hit;
180	  const block = world.get(x, y, z) as BlockId;
181	
182	  if (isHoe) {
183	    if (block !== BlockId.Grass && block !== BlockId.Dirt) return false;
184	    state.blockChanges.set(x, y, z, BlockId.Farmland);
185	    state.inventory = consumeToolDurability(state.inventory, state.selectedSlot, 1) ?? state.inventory;
186	    state.worldMeshDirty = true;
187	    emit({ type: "tilledSoil" });
188	    return true;
189	  }
190	
191	  // Seeds: plant on farmland when the cell above is clear.
192	  if (block !== BlockId.Farmland || world.get(x, y + 1, z) !== BlockId.Air) return false;
193	  state.blockChanges.set(x, y + 1, z, BlockId.WheatStage0);
194	  state.inventory = adjustSlotCount(state.inventory, slot.id, -1, state.selectedSlot) ?? state.inventory;
195	  state.worldMeshDirty = true;
196	  emit({ type: "plantedSeed" });
197	  return true;
198	}

Damage scales with power, so a\n// bigger blast both digs a wider crater and hits harder.\nexport const EXPLOSION_DAMAGE_PER_POWER = 6; // peak (point-blank) damage = power × this\nexport const CREEPER_EXPLOSION_POWER = 3; // crater radius in blocks\nexport const CREEPER_FUSE_SECONDS = 1.5; // hiss-to-detonation once primed\nexport const CREEPER_FUSE_RANGE = 2.6; // how close the player must be to light the fuse\nexport const CREEPER_ABORT_RANGE = 4.5; // walk past this while primed and the fuse aborts\nexport const TNT_EXPLOSION_POWER = 4; // a stronger blast than a creeper\nexport const TNT_FUSE_SECONDS = 2.5; // delay between igniting and detonating\n// A blast lights neighboring TNT after a short, randomized fuse so chains ripple.\nexport const TNT_CHAIN_FUSE_MIN_SECONDS = 0.1;\nexport const TNT_CHAIN_FUSE_MAX_SECONDS = 0.35;"

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/engine/systems/explosion.ts",
  "content": "import * as THREE from \"three\";\nimport { BlockId, doorState, isDoorBlock } from \"@/lib/world\";\nimport {\n  EXPLOSION_DAMAGE_PER_POWER,\n  TNT_CHAIN_FUSE_MAX_SECONDS,\n  TNT_CHAIN_FUSE_MIN_SECONDS,\n  TNT_EXPLOSION_POWER,\n  TNT_FUSE_SECONDS\n} from \"@/lib/game/config\";\nimport type { EmitGameEvent, GameState } from \"../state\";\n\n/**\n * Everything an explosion needs from the engine. A subset of `MobTickDeps`, so\n * the creeper branch in mobAI can pass its `deps` straight through.\n */\nexport type ExplosionDeps = {\n  applyDamage: (amount: number) => void;\n  rng: () => number;\n  emit: EmitGameEvent;\n};\n\n/** Blocks the blast can never break (mirrors the mining unbreakables + worldgen hazards). */\nfunction isBlastproof(block: BlockId): boolean {\n  return block === BlockId.Bedrock || block === BlockId.Spawner || block === BlockId.Lava;\n}\n\n/** Higher = harder to destroy: the blast strength at a cell must exceed this to break it. */\nfunction blastResistance(block: BlockId): number {\n  switch (block) {\n    case BlockId.Stone:\n    case BlockId.Cobblestone:\n    case BlockId.Brick:\n    case BlockId.MossyCobblestone:\n    case BlockId.Furnace:\n    case BlockId.CoalOre:\n    case BlockId.SliverOre:\n    case BlockId.RubyOre:\n    case BlockId.GoldOre:\n    case BlockId.SapphireOre:\n    case BlockId.DiamondOre:\n      return 2.5;\n    case BlockId.Chest:\n      return 1.2;\n    default:\n      return 0.4; // dirt, sand, wood, planks, leaves, glass, doors, crops, torches, ...\n  }\n}\n\nconst scratchKnock = new THREE.Vector3();\n\n/**\n * An explosion centered at (cx,cy,cz). It destroys blocks within `power` (a\n * distance falloff weighed against each block's blast resistance), then damages\n * the player and mobs out to twice that radius with the same falloff, and emits\n * one `explosion` event for the renderer/audio.\n *\n * It rides the existing single-block edit chokepoint: each destroyed cell goes\n * through `blockChanges.set` (which relights locally and records the save delta),\n * and `worldMeshDirty` is set **once** so the whole crater costs a single remesh.\n * It never removes mobs — it only lowers hp — so it is safe to call from inside\n * the mob tick loop; the caller sweeps any mob that reaches 0 hp.\n */\nexport function explode(state: GameState, cx: number, cy: number, cz: number, power: number, deps: ExplosionDeps): void {\n  const { world } = state;\n  const radius = Math.ceil(power);\n  const bx = Math.floor(cx);\n  const by = Math.floor(cy);\n  const bz = Math.floor(cz);\n  let changed = false;\n\n  for (let dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx += 1) {\n    for (let dy = -radius; dy <= radius; dy += 1) {\n      for (let dz = -radius; dz <= radius; dz += 1) {\n        const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);\n        if (dist > power) continue;\n        const x = bx + dx;\n        const y = by + dy;\n        const z = bz + dz;\n        if (!world.inBounds(x, y, z) || y <= 1) continue;\n        const block = world.get(x, y, z) as BlockId;\n        if (block === BlockId.Air || isBlastproof(block)) continue;\n        if (power * (1 - dist / power) <= blastResistance(block)) continue;\n\n        if (block === BlockId.Tnt) {\n          // Chain reaction: light neighboring TNT on a short randomized fuse\n          // instead of vaporizing it, so blasts ripple.\n          const idx = world.index(x, y, z);\n          if (!state.primedTnt.has(idx)) {\n            state.primedTnt.set(idx, TNT_CHAIN_FUSE_MIN_SECONDS + deps.rng() * (TNT_CHAIN_FUSE_MAX_SECONDS - TNT_CHAIN_FUSE_MIN_SECONDS));\n            deps.emit({ type: \"tntPrimed\", x, y, z });\n          }\n          continue;\n        }\n        if (isDoorBlock(block)) {\n          const door = doorState(block)!;\n          const otherY = door.upper ? y - 1 : y + 1;\n          state.blockChanges.set(x, y, z, BlockId.Air);\n          if (isDoorBlock(world.get(x, otherY, z) as BlockId)) state.blockChanges.set(x, otherY, z, BlockId.Air);\n        } else {\n          state.blockChanges.set(x, y, z, BlockId.Air);\n        }\n        changed = true;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  if (changed) state.worldMeshDirty = true;\n\n  const damageRange = power * 2;\n  const peak = power * EXPLOSION_DAMAGE_PER_POWER;\n\n  // Player: armor-aware damage + knockback away from the blast.\n  const { player } = state;\n  const pdist = Math.hypot(player.position.x - cx, player.position.y - cy, player.position.z - cz);\n  if (pdist < damageRange) {\n    const falloff = 1 - pdist / damageRange;\n    deps.applyDamage(Math.max(1, Math.round(peak * falloff)));\n    if (pdist > 0.001) {\n      scratchKnock.set(player.position.x - cx, player.position.y - cy, player.position.z - cz).normalize().multiplyScalar(power * falloff);\n      player.velocity.x += scratchKnock.x;\n      player.velocity.z += scratchKnock.z;\n      player.velocity.y = Math.max(player.velocity.y, Math.abs(scratchKnock.y) + 2);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Mobs: damage only — the caller removes any that reach 0 hp (so this is safe\n  // to run mid mob-loop). A light directional shove reads as the blast pushing them.\n  for (const mob of state.mobs) {\n    const mdist = Math.hypot(mob.position.x - cx, mob.position.y - cy, mob.position.z - cz);\n    if (mdist >= damageRange) continue;\n    mob.hp -= Math.max(1, Math.round(peak * (1 - mdist / damageRange)));\n    if (mdist > 0.001) mob.direction.set(mob.position.x - cx, 0, mob.position.z - cz).normalize();\n  }\n\n  deps.emit({ type: \"explosion\", x: cx, y: cy, z: cz, power });\n}\n\n/** Lights the TNT block at (x,y,z) on a full fuse (no-op if already lit). */\nexport function primeTnt(state: GameState, x: number, y: number, z: number, emit: EmitGameEvent): void {\n  const idx = state.world.index(x, y, z);\n  if (state.primedTnt.has(idx)) return;\n  state.primedTnt.set(idx, TNT_FUSE_SECONDS);\n  emit({ type: \"tntPrimed\", x, y, z });\n}\n\n/**\n * Counts down lit TNT and detonates it. A TNT block mined or replaced before its\n * fuse runs out is silently dropped (the block check). Detonations run after the\n * countdown pass so a blast's chain-priming can't mutate the map mid-iteration.\n */\nexport function tickPrimedTnt(state: GameState, dt: number, deps: ExplosionDeps): void {\n  if (state.primedTnt.size === 0) return;\n  const { world } = state;\n  const layer = world.sizeX * world.sizeZ;\n  const detonations: Array<[number, number, number]> = [];\n\n  for (const [idx, timer] of state.primedTnt) {\n    const y = Math.floor(idx / layer);\n    const rem = idx - y * layer;\n    const z = Math.floor(rem / world.sizeX);\n    const x = rem - z * world.sizeX;\n    if (world.get(x, y, z) !== BlockId.Tnt) {\n      state.primedTnt.delete(idx); // mined or overwritten → forget it\n      continue;\n    }\n    const next = timer - dt;\n    if (next > 0) {\n      state.primedTnt.set(idx, next);\n    } else {\n      state.primedTnt.delete(idx);\n      detonations.push([x, y, z]);\n    }\n  }\n\n  for (const [x, y, z] of detonations) {\n    state.blockChanges.set(x, y, z, BlockId.Air);\n    state.worldMeshDirty = true;\n    explode(state, x + 0.5, y + 0.5, z + 0.5, TNT_EXPLOSION_POWER, deps);\n  }\n}\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/engine/systems/mobAI.ts",
  "old_string": "    // The boss conjures minions while it is engaged with the player.\n    if (!isDead && isBoss && activeHostile && distanceToPlayer < mob.detectRange) {\n      tickBossSummon(state, mob, dt, deps);\n    }\n\n    // Zombies and skeletons burn in broad daylight; the boss is immune.\n    if (mob.hostile && mob.kind !== \"spider\" && mob.kind !== \"boss\" && daylight > HOSTILE_BURN_ABOVE_DAYLIGHT) {\n      mob.hp -= dt * 0.8;\n    }\n\n    if (mob.hp <= 0) deadIndices.push(i);\n  }\n\n  for (let i = deadIndices.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) deps.removeMobAt(deadIndices[i]);\n}",
  "new_string": "    // The boss conjures minions while it is engaged with the player.\n    if (!isDead && isBoss && activeHostile && distanceToPlayer < mob.detectRange) {\n      tickBossSummon(state, mob, dt, deps);\n    }\n\n    // A creeper lights its fuse when it gets close, then detonates — destroying\n    // terrain and hurting the player and nearby mobs. Walk out of range while it\n    // is primed and the fuse aborts. It dies in its own blast (dropping gunpowder\n    // via the post-loop sweep); killing it before the fuse runs out cancels it.\n    if (!isDead && mob.kind === \"creeper\") {\n      if ((mob.fuseTimer ?? 0) > 0) {\n        if (!activeHostile || distanceToPlayer > CREEPER_ABORT_RANGE) {\n          mob.fuseTimer = 0;\n        } else {\n          mob.fuseTimer = (mob.fuseTimer ?? 0) - dt;\n          if (mob.fuseTimer <= 0) {\n            explode(state, mob.position.x, mob.position.y, mob.position.z, CREEPER_EXPLOSION_POWER, deps);\n            mob.hp = 0;\n          }\n        }\n      } else if (activeHostile && attackDistance < CREEPER_FUSE_RANGE && hasLineOfSight) {\n        mob.fuseTimer = CREEPER_FUSE_SECONDS;\n        deps.emit({ type: \"mobAttacked\", kind: \"creeper\" }); // the hiss\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Zombies, skeletons, and creepers burn in broad daylight; the boss is immune.\n    if (mob.hostile && mob.kind !== \"spider\" && mob.kind !== \"boss\" && daylight > HOSTILE_BURN_ABOVE_DAYLIGHT) {\n      mob.hp -= dt * 0.8;\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Sweep the dead after the loop (descending, so splices don't shift live\n  // indices). A post-loop sweep — not per-iteration — also catches mobs killed by\n  // an explosion earlier in the same pass, including the creeper that just blew up.\n  for (let i = mobs.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {\n    if (mobs[i].hp <= 0) deps.removeMobAt(i);\n  }\n}"
}

763	  return MATERIAL_PALETTES[prefix] ?? STEEL;

766	/** 16x16 RGBA pixels for any item id; magenta checker for unknown ids. */
767	export function renderSpritePixels(itemId: string): Uint8ClampedArray {
768	  const def = ITEM_DEF_BY_ID[itemId];
769	  const custom = ITEM_SPRITE_GRIDS[itemId];
770	  if (custom) return paintGrid(custom.grid, custom.palette);
771	  if (def?.kind === "block" && def.blockId !== undefined) return paintIsoBlock(def.blockId);
772	  if (def?.kind === "tool" && itemId.endsWith("_hoe")) return paintGrid(HOE_GRID, toolPalette(materialFor(itemId)));
773	  if (def?.kind === "tool") return paintGrid(PICKAXE_GRID, toolPalette(materialFor(itemId)));
774	  if (def?.kind === "weapon") {
775	    if (itemId === "knife") return paintGrid(KNIFE_GRID, toolPalette(STEEL));
776	    if (itemId.endsWith("_spear")) return paintGrid(SPEAR_GRID, toolPalette(materialFor(itemId)));
777	    return paintGrid(SWORD_GRID, toolPalette(materialFor(itemId)));
778	  }
779	  if (def?.kind === "armor" && def.armorSlot) {
780	    return paintGrid(ARMOR_GRIDS[def.armorSlot] ?? UNKNOWN_GRID, { m: STEEL.m, M: STEEL.M, l: STEEL.l });
781	  }
782	  return paintGrid(UNKNOWN_GRID, { p: [240, 40, 240], P: [40, 8, 40] });
783	}

```
1	import * as THREE from "three";
2	import { BABY_SCALE } from "@/lib/game/config";
3	import { createMobModelForKind } from "@/lib/game/mobs";
4	import type { MobModel } from "@/lib/game/types";
5	import type { MobState } from "@/lib/game/engine/state";
6	
7	export type MobVisuals = {
8	  /** Creates/removes models to match the mob list and animates bob + gait. */
9	  sync(mobs: MobState[], timeMs: number): void;
10	  dispose(): void;
11	};
12	
13	export function createMobVisuals(scene: THREE.Scene): MobVisuals {
14	  const models = new Map<number, MobModel>();
15	  const seen = new Set<number>();
16	
17	  const removeModel = (id: number) => {
18	    const model = models.get(id);
19	    if (!model) return;
20	    scene.remove(model.group);
21	    for (const material of model.materials) material.dispose();
22	    for (const geometry of model.geometries) geometry.dispose();
23	    models.delete(id);
24	  };
25	
26	  return {
27	    sync(mobs, timeMs) {
28	      seen.clear();
29	      for (const mob of mobs) {
30	        seen.add(mob.id);
31	        let model = models.get(mob.id);
32	        if (!model) {
33	          model = createMobModelForKind(mob.kind);
34	          models.set(mob.id, model);
35	          scene.add(model.group);
36	        }
37	
38	        const bob = Math.sin(timeMs * 0.008 + mob.bobSeed) * 0.04;
39	        model.group.position.set(mob.position.x, mob.position.y + bob, mob.position.z);
40	        model.group.rotation.y = mob.yaw;
41	        model.group.scale.setScalar(mob.ageTimer > 0 ? BABY_SCALE : 1);
42	
43	        const gait = Math.sin(timeMs * 0.015 * mob.moveSpeed + mob.bobSeed) * 0.3;
44	        if (model.legs.length === 4) {
45	          model.legs[0].rotation.x = gait;
46	          model.legs[1].rotation.x = -gait;
47	          model.legs[2].rotation.x = -gait;
48	          model.legs[3].rotation.x = gait;
49	        }
50	      }
51	
52	      for (const id of models.keys()) {
53	        if (!seen.has(id)) removeModel(id);
54	      }
55	    },
56	
57	    dispose() {
58	      for (const id of [...models.keys()]) removeModel(id);
59	    }
60	  };
61	}
62	
```

71	export function randomLandPointNear(
72	  world: VoxelWorld,
73	  surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn,
74	  centerX: number,
75	  centerZ: number,
76	  radius: number,
77	  rng: () => number = Math.random
78	): THREE.Vector3 {
79	  for (let i = 0; i < 50; i += 1) {
80	    const x = centerX + (rng() * 2 - 1) * radius;
81	    const z = centerZ + (rng() * 2 - 1) * radius;
82	    const clampedX = Math.max(10, Math.min(world.sizeX - 10, x));
83	    const clampedZ = Math.max(10, Math.min(world.sizeZ - 10, z));
84	    const y = surfaceYAt(clampedX, clampedZ);
85	    if (y > 2 && isDrySurface(world, clampedX, y, clampedZ)) return new THREE.Vector3(clampedX, y, clampedZ);
86	  }
87	  return randomLandPoint(world, surfaceYAt, rng);
88	}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/spawn.ts",
  "old_string": "export function randomLandPointNear(\n  world: VoxelWorld,\n  surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn,\n  centerX: number,\n  centerZ: number,\n  radius: number,\n  rng: () => number = Math.random\n): THREE.Vector3 {\n  for (let i = 0; i < 50; i += 1) {\n    const x = centerX + (rng() * 2 - 1) * radius;\n    const z = centerZ + (rng() * 2 - 1) * radius;\n    const clampedX = Math.max(10, Math.min(world.sizeX - 10, x));\n    const clampedZ = Math.max(10, Math.min(world.sizeZ - 10, z));\n    const y = surfaceYAt(clampedX, clampedZ);\n    if (y > 2 && isDrySurface(world, clampedX, y, clampedZ)) return new THREE.Vector3(clampedX, y, clampedZ);\n  }\n  return randomLandPoint(world, surfaceYAt, rng);\n}",
  "new_string": "export function randomLandPointNear(\n  world: VoxelWorld,\n  surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn,\n  centerX: number,\n  centerZ: number,\n  radius: number,\n  rng: () => number = Math.random,\n  minRadius = 0\n): THREE.Vector3 {\n  for (let i = 0; i < 50; i += 1) {\n    const x = centerX + (rng() * 2 - 1) * radius;\n    const z = centerZ + (rng() * 2 - 1) * radius;\n    const clampedX = Math.max(10, Math.min(world.sizeX - 10, x));\n    const clampedZ = Math.max(10, Math.min(world.sizeZ - 10, z));\n    // Keep a minimum standoff so a mob can't spawn on top of the player.\n    if (minRadius > 0 && Math.hypot(clampedX - centerX, clampedZ - centerZ) < minRadius) continue;\n    const y = surfaceYAt(clampedX, clampedZ);\n    if (y > 2 && isDrySurface(world, clampedX, y, clampedZ)) return new THREE.Vector3(clampedX, y, clampedZ);\n  }\n  return randomLandPoint(world, surfaceYAt, rng);\n}"
}
```

  "old_string": "export function spawnMobGroup(state: GameState, args: SpawnGroupArgs, rng: () => number, surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn): void {\n  for (let i = 0; i < args.count; i += 1) {\n    const spawnPos = randomLandPointNear(state.world, surfaceYAt, args.centerX, args.centerZ, args.radius, rng);\n    pushMob(state, args.kind, args.hostile, spawnPos.x, spawnPos.y, spawnPos.z, rng);\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "export function spawnMobGroup(state: GameState, args: SpawnGroupArgs, rng: () => number, surfaceYAt: SurfaceYAtFn): void {\n  for (let i = 0; i < args.count; i += 1) {\n    const spawnPos = randomLandPointNear(state.world, surfaceYAt, args.centerX, args.centerZ, args.radius, rng, args.minRadius ?? 0);\n    pushMob(state, args.kind, args.hostile, spawnPos.x, spawnPos.y, spawnPos.z, rng);\n  }\n}"
}

  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/david/monecraft/lib/game/engine/systems/explosion.test.ts",
  "content": "import { describe, expect, test } from \"bun:test\";\nimport * as THREE from \"three\";\nimport { BlockId, VoxelWorld } from \"@/lib/world\";\nimport { TNT_FUSE_SECONDS } from \"@/lib/game/config\";\nimport { createBlockChangeTracker } from \"@/lib/game/engine/blockChanges\";\nimport { createTimers, type GameEvent, type GameState, type MobState } from \"@/lib/game/engine/state\";\nimport { explode, primeTnt, tickPrimedTnt } from \"@/lib/game/engine/systems/explosion\";\n\n/** A 24³ world whose lower half is solid dirt — soft blocks the blast can clear. */\nfunction makeWorld(): VoxelWorld {\n  const world = new VoxelWorld(24, 24, 24, 1);\n  for (let x = 0; x < world.sizeX; x += 1) {\n    for (let z = 0; z < world.sizeZ; z += 1) {\n      for (let y = 0; y < 12; y += 1) world.set(x, y, z, BlockId.Dirt);\n    }\n  }\n  return world;\n}\n\nfunction makeMob(x: number, y: number, z: number, hp = 20): MobState {\n  return {\n    id: 1,\n    kind: \"zombie\",\n    hostile: true,\n    hp,\n    position: new THREE.Vector3(x, y, z),\n    direction: new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0),\n    yaw: 0,\n    turnTimer: 0,\n    speed: 1,\n    moveSpeed: 1,\n    detectRange: 11,\n    attackDamage: 3,\n    attackCooldown: 1,\n    attackTimer: 0,\n    halfHeight: 0.9,\n    bobSeed: 0,\n    fedTimer: 0,\n    ageTimer: 0\n  };\n}\n\nfunction makeState(world: VoxelWorld, mobs: MobState[] = []): GameState {\n  return {\n    world,\n    blockChanges: createBlockChangeTracker(world),\n    player: { position: new THREE.Vector3(0, 64, 0), velocity: new THREE.Vector3(), yaw: 0, pitch: 0, onGround: true },\n    mobs,\n    primedTnt: new Map<number, number>(),\n    worldMeshDirty: false,\n    timers: createTimers()\n  } as unknown as GameState;\n}\n\nfunction deps(events: GameEvent[], damages: number[]) {\n  return {\n    applyDamage: (amount: number) => damages.push(amount),\n    rng: () => 0.5,\n    emit: (e: GameEvent) => events.push(e)\n  };\n}\n\ndescribe(\"explode — block destruction\", () => {\n  test(\"clears a sphere of soft blocks and sets the remesh flag once, emitting one event\", () => {\n    const world = makeWorld();\n    const state = makeState(world);\n    const events: GameEvent[] = [];\n    explode(state, 8.5, 8.5, 8.5, 3, deps(events, []));\n\n    expect(world.get(8, 8, 8)).toBe(BlockId.Air); // center cleared\n    expect(world.get(8, 11, 8)).toBe(BlockId.Air); // within radius 3\n    expect(world.get(8, 4, 8)).toBe(BlockId.Dirt); // distance 4 > power 3 — untouched\n    expect(state.worldMeshDirty).toBe(true);\n    expect(events.filter((e) => e.type === \"explosion\")).toHaveLength(1);\n  });\n\n  test(\"never breaks bedrock or a spawner inside the blast\", () => {\n    const world = makeWorld();\n    world.set(8, 8, 9, BlockId.Bedrock);\n    world.set(9, 8, 8, BlockId.Spawner);\n    const state = makeState(world);\n    explode(state, 8.5, 8.5, 8.5, 3, deps([], []));\n\n    expect(world.get(8, 8, 9)).toBe(BlockId.Bedrock);\n    expect(world.get(9, 8, 8)).toBe(BlockId.Spawner);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"explode — damage falloff\", () => {\n  test(\"damages the player most at the center and less at the edge\", () => {\n    const near: number[] = [];\n    const far: number[] = [];\n    const sNear = makeState(makeWorld());\n    sNear.player.position.set(8.5, 8.5, 8.5);\n    explode(sNear, 8.5, 8.5, 8.5, 3, deps([], near));\n\n    const sFar = makeState(makeWorld());\n    sFar.player.position.set(8.5 + 5, 8.5, 8.5); // dist 5, within range (power*2 = 6)\n    explode(sFar, 8.5, 8.5, 8.5, 3, deps([], far));\n\n    expect(near[0]).toBeGreaterThan(far[0]);\n    expect(far[0]).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1); // still inside the damage radius\n  });\n\n  test(\"leaves a player outside the damage radius unharmed and applies knockback inside it\", () => {\n    const damages: number[] = [];\n    const state = makeState(makeWorld());\n    state.player.position.set(8.5 + 7, 8.5, 8.5); // dist 7 > power*2 = 6\n    explode(state, 8.5, 8.5, 8.5, 3, deps([], damages));\n    expect(damages).toHaveLength(0);\n\n    const hit = makeState(makeWorld());\n    hit.player.position.set(8.5 + 2, 8.5, 8.5);\n    explode(hit, 8.5, 8.5, 8.5, 3, deps([], []));\n    expect(hit.player.velocity.x).toBeGreaterThan(0); // shoved away from the blast\n  });\n\n  test(\"damages mobs in range without removing them (caller sweeps the dead)\", () => {\n    const mob = makeMob(9.5, 8.5, 8.5, 20);\n    const state = makeState(makeWorld(), [mob]);\n    explode(state, 8.5, 8.5, 8.5, 3, deps([], []));\n    expect(mob.hp).toBeLessThan(20);\n    expect(state.mobs).toHaveLength(1); // explode only lowers hp; it never splices\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"primed TNT\", () => {\n  test(\"primeTnt lights a fuse and tickPrimedTnt detonates it when the fuse runs out\", () => {\n    const world = makeWorld();\n    world.set(8, 12, 8, BlockId.Tnt); // a TNT block sitting on the dirt\n    const state = makeState(world);\n    const events: GameEvent[] = [];\n    const d = deps(events, []);\n\n    primeTnt(state, 8, 12, 8, d.emit);\n    expect(state.primedTnt.size).toBe(1);\n    expect(events.some((e) => e.type === \"tntPrimed\")).toBe(true);\n\n    tickPrimedTnt(state, TNT_FUSE_SECONDS - 0.1, d); // not yet\n    expect(state.primedTnt.size).toBe(1);\n    expect(world.get(8, 12, 8)).toBe(BlockId.Tnt);\n\n    tickPrimedTnt(state, 0.2, d); // fuse expires\n    expect(world.get(8, 12, 8)).toBe(BlockId.Air);\n    expect(state.primedTnt.size).toBe(0);\n    expect(events.some((e) => e.type === \"explosion\")).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  test(\"a blast chains adjacent TNT by lighting it rather than vaporizing it\", () => {\n    const world = makeWorld();\n    world.set(10, 12, 8, BlockId.Tnt); // adjacent to the blast, within radius\n    const state = makeState(world);\n    explode(state, 9.5, 12.5, 8.5, 4, deps([], []));\n\n    // The neighboring TNT survives the blast but is now primed (will blow next).\n    expect(world.get(10, 12, 8)).toBe(BlockId.Tnt);\n    expect(state.primedTnt.has(world.index(10, 12, 8))).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  test(\"a primed TNT block that was mined away never detonates\", () => {\n    const world = makeWorld();\n    world.set(8, 12, 8, BlockId.Tnt);\n    const state = makeState(world);\n    primeTnt(state, 8, 12, 8, () => {});\n    world.set(8, 12, 8, BlockId.Air); // mined before the fuse ran out\n\n    const events: GameEvent[] = [];\n    tickPrimedTnt(state, TNT_FUSE_SECONDS + 1, deps(events, []));\n    expect(state.primedTnt.size).toBe(0);\n    expect(events.some((e) => e.type === \"explosion\")).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n"
}

33	  return {
34	    world: new VoxelWorld(48, 48, 48, 1),
35	    mobs,
36	    projectiles: [],
37	    nextProjectileId: 1,
38	    daylight: 0.1, // night-ish so hostiles stay active and don't burn

40	  } as unknown as GameState;

43	function makeDeps() {
44	  const events: GameEvent[] = [];
45	  let damage = 0;
46	  const deps: MobTickDeps = {
47	    surfaceYAt: () => 29, // keeps mob.y ≈ player.y so the vertical gap stays small
48	    applyDamage: (a: number) => {
49	      damage += a;
50	    },
51	    removeMobAt: () => {},
52	    rng: () => 0.5,
53	    emit: (e: GameEvent) => events.push(e)
54	  };
55	  return { deps, events, getDamage: () => damage };
56	}
57

59	  test("a skeleton with line of sight fires an arrow and re-arms its cooldown", () => {

61	    const state = makeState([skeleton]);
62	    const { deps, events } = makeDeps();
63	
64	    tickMobs(state, 0.05, deps);
65	
66	    expect(state.projectiles).toHaveLength(1);
67	    expect(state.projectiles[0].fromPlayer).toBe(false);
68	    expect(skeleton.attackTimer).toBeCloseTo(skeleton.attackCooldown, 5);
69	    expect(events.some((e) => e.type === "mobAttacked" && e.kind === "skeleton")).toBe(true);
70	  });

  "new_string": "  test(\"a zombie still melees and shoots nothing\", () => {\n    const zombie = makeMob(\"zombie\", 24, 30, 21.5); // ~2.5 blocks, within melee reach\n    const state = makeState([zombie]);\n    const { deps, events, getDamage } = makeDeps();\n\n    tickMobs(state, 0.05, deps);\n\n    expect(state.projectiles).toHaveLength(0);\n    expect(getDamage()).toBe(3); // melee damage applied\n    expect(events.some((e) => e.type === \"mobAttacked\" && e.kind === \"zombie\")).toBe(true);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"creepers\", () => {\n  function makeCreeper(z: number): MobState {\n    const c = makeMob(\"creeper\", 24, 30, z);\n    c.hp = 10;\n    c.attackDamage = 0;\n    c.detectRange = 12;\n    return c;\n  }\n\n  test(\"lights its fuse (hissing, no melee) when the player gets close\", () => {\n    const creeper = makeCreeper(22.5); // ~1.5 blocks, inside fuse range\n    const state = makeState([creeper]);\n    const { deps, events, getDamage } = makeDeps();\n\n    tickMobs(state, 0.05, deps);\n\n    expect(creeper.fuseTimer).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(getDamage()).toBe(0); // it never bites\n    expect(events.some((e) => e.type === \"mobAttacked\" && e.kind === \"creeper\")).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  test(\"detonates when the fuse runs out — exploding, dealing damage, and dying\", () => {\n    const creeper = makeCreeper(22.5);\n    const state = makeState([creeper]);\n    const { deps, events, getDamage } = makeDeps();\n\n    tickMobs(state, 0.05, deps); // light the fuse\n    for (let t = 0; t < CREEPER_FUSE_SECONDS + 0.2; t += 0.1) tickMobs(state, 0.1, deps);\n\n    expect(events.some((e) => e.type === \"explosion\")).toBe(true);\n    expect(getDamage()).toBeGreaterThan(0); // the blast hurt the player\n    expect(creeper.hp).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0); // it dies in its own blast (sweep drops gunpowder)\n  });\n\n  test(\"aborts the fuse when the player backs out of range\", () => {\n    const creeper = makeCreeper(22.5);\n    const state = makeState([creeper]);\n    const { deps } = makeDeps();\n\n    tickMobs(state, 0.05, deps);\n    expect(creeper.fuseTimer).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n\n    state.player.position.set(24, 30, 8); // walk far away (> abort range)\n    tickMobs(state, 0.05, deps);\n    expect(creeper.fuseTimer).toBe(0);\n  });\n\n  test(\"removes a creeper killed before its fuse finishes (no explosion)\", () => {\n    const creeper = makeCreeper(22.5);\n    const state = makeState([creeper]);\n    const removed: number[] = [];\n    const { deps, events } = makeDeps();\n    deps.removeMobAt = (i: number) => removed.push(i);\n\n    tickMobs(state, 0.05, deps); // primes the fuse\n    creeper.hp = 0; // killed by a sword/arrow before it detonates\n    tickMobs(state, 0.05, deps);\n\n    expect(removed).toContain(0); // swept (drops gunpowder via the engine's removeMobAt)\n    expect(events.some((e) => e.type === \"explosion\")).toBe(false);\n  });\n});"
}

  "new_string": "    const boss = engine.state.mobs.find((mob) => mob.kind === \"boss\")!;\n    const hpBefore = boss.hp;\n    run(engine, 2);\n    expect(engine.state.daylight).toBeGreaterThan(0.72);\n    expect(boss.hp).toBe(hpBefore); // immune to the daylight burn\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"TNT\", () => {\n  test(\"igniting placed TNT with a torch blows it up after its fuse\", () => {\n    const engine = makeEngine();\n    calmDaytime(engine);\n    engine.state.mobs = [];\n    run(engine, 1);\n    const { state } = engine;\n\n    // Aim at a TNT block one cell ahead at eye height.\n    const x = Math.floor(state.player.position.x);\n    const z = Math.floor(state.player.position.z) - 1;\n    const y = Math.floor(state.player.position.y + EYE_HEIGHT);\n    state.player.position.x = x + 0.5;\n    state.player.position.z = z + 1.5;\n    state.player.yaw = 0;\n    state.player.pitch = 0;\n    state.blockChanges.set(x, y, z, BlockId.Tnt);\n\n    // Hold a torch and right-click the TNT to light it (the torch is not consumed).\n    state.inventory = [...state.inventory];\n    state.inventory[0] = createSlot(\"torch\", 1);\n    state.selectedSlot = 0;\n    engine.consumeEvents();\n    engine.dispatch({ type: \"placeBlock\" });\n\n    expect(engine.consumeEvents().some((e) => e.type === \"tntPrimed\")).toBe(true);\n    expect(state.primedTnt.size).toBe(1);\n    expect(countsById(state.inventory).get(\"torch\")).toBe(1); // torch survives\n\n    // Run past the fuse; the TNT detonates, clearing itself and firing an explosion.\n    run(engine, 3);\n    expect(state.world.get(x, y, z)).toBe(BlockId.Air);\n    expect(state.primedTnt.size).toBe(0);\n  });\n});"

Back away to defuse one, or kill it first for its gunpowder.\n  Spiders are only hostile in the dark. Nothing spawns within 16 blocks of you."
}

196	wood, leaves, and crops need no special tool. Ores hide underground and in caves;
197	the rarer the ore, the deeper it tends to sit.
198	
199	## Dungeons and loot
200	
201	Digging around underground can turn up a **dungeon**: a small cobblestone room,
202	flecked with mossy cobble, sealed in the rock away from your spawn. Inside are
203	**loot chests** — open them for a free haul of food, ores, and sometimes real
204	gear (a sword, armor, even a diamond pickaxe if you're lucky). Each chest's loot
205	is fixed until you reach it, and once you've opened (or broken) it, it won't
206	refill — so there's no farming the same chest twice.
207	
```

  "new_string": "wood, leaves, and crops need no special tool. Ores hide underground and in caves;\nthe rarer the ore, the deeper it tends to sit.\n\n**Blasting with TNT.** Collect **gunpowder** from creepers and craft **TNT** (4\ngunpowder + 1 sand). Place it like any block, then **right-click it with a torch**\nto light the fuse — stand well back, because after a couple of seconds it blows a\ncrater and hurts anything close. TNT next to more TNT chains. It won't touch\nbedrock, and it mostly destroys rather than drops blocks, so it's for digging and\ndemolition, not free resources.\n\n## Dungeons and loot"
}

70	## Danger — day-night & the mob director
71	
72	`DAY_CYCLE_SECONDS`, `HOSTILE_SPAWN_BELOW_DAYLIGHT`, `SPIDER_AGGRO_BELOW_DAYLIGHT`,
73	`HOSTILE_BURN_ABOVE_DAYLIGHT`, `HOSTILE_SPAWN_INTERVAL_SECONDS`, `HOSTILE_CAP`,
74	`SPAWNER_INTERVAL_SECONDS`, `SPAWNER_ACTIVATION_RADIUS`, `SPAWNER_LOCAL_CAP`.
75	
76	Read by `systems/dayNight.ts`, `systems/spawnDirector.ts`, and `systems/mobAI.ts`.
77	`DAY_CYCLE_SECONDS` (240) sets the whole rhythm — a shorter day means more frequent
78	nights and more pressure. The three daylight thresholds are a **documented engine
79	invariant** (daylight ranges 0.04–1.0; see architecture.md): hostiles spawn below
80	0.28, spiders turn hostile below 0.42, and exposed hostiles burn above 0.72. Keep
81	them ordered `spawn ≤ spider_aggro` and `burn` well above both, or mobs will spawn
82	into instant sunlight. `HOSTILE_CAP` × `HOSTILE_SPAWN_INTERVAL_SECONDS` bounds how
83	crowded a night gets. Tests aim daylight explicitly (a `calmDaytime` helper) to
84	avoid first-night aggro.
85	
86	**Dungeon spawners** are a separate, time-independent danger source: while the
87	player is within `SPAWNER_ACTIVATION_RADIUS` (16) of an intact spawner, it drips
88	one hostile every `SPAWNER_INTERVAL_SECONDS` (8) up to `SPAWNER_LOCAL_CAP` (6)
89	clustered nearby — all still under the shared `HOSTILE_CAP`. Lower the interval or

A blast's **power** is its\nblock-destruction radius; it also damages out to **twice** that radius. Peak\n(point-blank) damage is `power × EXPLOSION_DAMAGE_PER_POWER` (6), tapering linearly\nto the edge — so raising a power both widens the crater and hits harder. Per-block\n`blastResistance` (which blocks survive a given strength) and the unbreakable set\n(bedrock/spawner/lava) live in `explosion.ts`, not config. `CREEPER_FUSE_RANGE`\narms the fuse; `CREEPER_ABORT_RANGE` (larger) defuses it when you flee. `TNT_*`\nfuses gate the lit delay and the randomized chain delay that ripples one blast into\nthe next. Damage uses the armor-aware path, so armor still mitigates a blast.\n\n## Farming & breeding pace"
}

  "new_string": "### Version history\n\n- **v5** (unchanged for creepers & TNT) — the creeper mob, gunpowder, and the explosion system add no persisted state: mobs are transient, **a creeper's lit fuse and a primed TNT block's countdown are session-only** (`MobState.fuseTimer` and `state.primedTnt` are never serialized — like the boss and in-flight arrows). Gunpowder and the TNT item save by string id; a **placed TNT block persists in the block diff** like any block, and a save written mid-fuse reloads with that TNT as an inert, un-lit block (and no creeper). There is no worldgen change — creepers are spawned and TNT is crafted — so neither the `version` nor `WORLDGEN_VERSION` moved. Blast-destroyed blocks are ordinary block-diff edits.\n- **v5** (unchanged for cow/pig & coal) —"
}

5:## A new block
16:## A new item or recipe
30:## A new mob drop
32:- Mob loot lives in `lib/game/mobLoot.ts`: add or edit the `MOB_DROPS[kind]` entries (`{ itemId, min, max, chance? }`). `rollMobDrops` is the single roll, called once from `GameEngine.removeMobAt` for every death (combat kills and daylight burns alike), so there is nothing else to wire.
33:- Every `itemId` must exist in `ITEM_DEFS`; `lib/game/mobLoot.test.ts` enforces that and the count bounds.
35:## A new mob
37:- Add a template to `MOB_TEMPLATES` in `lib/game/mobs.ts` — `detectRange: 0` means passive (wanders, flees the player), `> 0` means hostile (chases, attacks with line-of-sight check).
38:- The model is assembled from `createMobModel(...)` color/size args in `lib/game/mobModel.ts` (legs animate automatically in `lib/game/render/mobVisuals.ts`).
39:- Wire spawning in `lib/game/engine/systems/spawnDirector.ts`: `spawnInitialMobs` for the day-one population, `tickHostileSpawnDirector` for the night respawn loop.
40:- Give it a voice: `MOB_AMBIENT_SOUNDS` and `MOB_ATTACK_SOUNDS` rows in `lib/game/audio/soundParams.ts`, and a call interval in `lib/game/audio/mobAmbience.ts` (`CALL_INTERVALS`) — all keyed by `MobKind`, so typecheck enforces them.
41:- A headless test in `lib/game/engine/GameEngine.test.ts` is cheap: boot the engine, fast-forward to night, assert the mob appears/behaves.
42:- To make a passive animal **breedable**, add it to `FEED_ITEMS` in `lib/game/engine/systems/interact.ts` (which food puts it "in love"). Breeding itself is generic: `lib/game/engine/systems/breeding.ts` pairs two fed adults of the same kind into a baby (scaled down via `ageTimer`/`BABY_SCALE`, no drops until grown), bounded by `PASSIVE_CAP`. `MobState.fedTimer`/`ageTimer` and the tunables in `config.ts` drive it; mobs are never persisted, so this is session-only by design.
43:- **A new `MobKind` touches five exhaustive `Record<MobKind>` tables** (typecheck enforces all of them): `MOB_TEMPLATES` (`mobs.ts`), `MOB_DROPS` (`mobLoot.ts`), `CALL_INTERVALS` (`mobAmbience.ts`), and `MOB_AMBIENT_SOUNDS` + `MOB_ATTACK_SOUNDS` (`soundParams.ts`). `soundParams.test.ts` iterates `MOB_TEMPLATES`, so the three sound/template tables must stay in lockstep.
45:## A ranged weapon or ranged mob
47:- Arrows ride the shared **projectile system** (`lib/game/engine/projectiles.ts`, `engine/systems/projectileAI.ts`): call `spawnArrow(state, x, y, z, dir, { speed, damage, knockback, fromPlayer, ttl })`. `fromPlayer: true` hits mobs, `false` hits the player; arrows never hit their firer. Projectiles are session-only `ProjectileState` (never serialized).
49:- A **ranged mob** sets `ranged: true` on its `MobTemplate`; `mobAI.ts` then makes it kite (a standoff band) and fire toward the player's chest (with simple lead) instead of meleeing. The boss is the special case there: it approaches, melees up close, fires a spread, and summons minions — see `fireBossSpread` / `tickBossSummon`.
52:## A new player skin preset
58:## A new sound
62:- Coverage is pinned: `materials.test.ts` and `soundParams.test.ts` fail on missing material/mob rows, and routing is testable headlessly with the fake `SynthBackend` pattern in `audioDirector.test.ts`. Actual sound quality is a manual `bun run dev` pass.
64:## An interactive block (right-click behavior)
66:- Right-click (and KeyE) dispatch `placeBlock`, which runs a fixed precedence in `GameEngine.dispatch` before falling through to placement: feed an aimed mob → `tryInteractBlock` → use the held item → place. To make a block do something on right-click, register it in `INTERACTIVE_BLOCKS` and add a branch in `tryInteractBlock` (`lib/game/engine/systems/interact.ts`).
70:## A container block (block-entity storage)
78:## A random-tick behavior (growth / spread)
84:## A worldgen structure (houses, dungeons, …)
90:## A loot table / lazy block-entity fill
92:- Drop tables are pure data + a roll function: copy the `{ itemId, min, max, chance? }` shape and the `clampUnit` roll loop from `lib/game/mobLoot.ts` / `lib/game/dungeonLoot.ts`. Add a `*.test.ts` asserting every `itemId` exists in `ITEM_DEFS` and that rolls are deterministic under an injected rng.
95:## A new mechanic

45	## A ranged weapon or ranged mob
46	
47	- Arrows ride the shared **projectile system** (`lib/game/engine/projectiles.ts`, `engine/systems/projectileAI.ts`): call `spawnArrow(state, x, y, z, dir, { speed, damage, knockback, fromPlayer, ttl })`. `fromPlayer: true` hits mobs, `false` hits the player; arrows never hit their firer. Projectiles are session-only `ProjectileState` (never serialized).
48	- A **player ranged weapon** branches the `attack` command: gate on the held item (see `isBow`/`tryFireBow` in `combat.ts`) and fire instead of meleeing. Use a `config.ts` cooldown timer (`GameTimers`, decremented in `GameEngine.step`) for fire rate, and spend ammo + durability via `adjustSlotCount` / `consumeToolDurability`.
49	- A **ranged mob** sets `ranged: true` on its `MobTemplate`; `mobAI.ts` then makes it kite (a standoff band) and fire toward the player's chest (with simple lead) instead of meleeing. The boss is the special case there: it approaches, melees up close, fires a spread, and summons minions — see `fireBossSpread` / `tickBossSummon`.
50	- The renderer auto-renders any arrow via `projectileVisuals.ts` (a pooled extruded `arrow` sprite); add an impact event (e.g. `arrowHit`) for particles/sound if you want feedback.

{
  "replace_all": false,
